vivecana-mūlasūtra meṃ ina pratimāoṃ ke nāma-nirdeśa nahīṃ hai, ataḥ daśāśrutaskandha-niyukti ke anusāra unakā kucha vivaraṇa kiyā jātā hai - mūla meṃ pratimāe~ pā~ca kahī gaī hai-samādhipratimā, upadhānapratimā, vivekapratimā, pratisaṃlīnatāpratimā aura ekākīvihārapratimā| inameṃ samādhipratimā do prakāra kī hai - śrutasamādhipratimā aura cāritrasamādhipratimā| darśanapratimā ko bhinna nahīṃ kahā kyoṃki usakā jñāna meṃ antarbhāva ho jātā hai| śrutasamādhipratimā ke bāsaṭha bheda haiṃ - ācārāṅga ke prathama śrutaskandha-gata pā~ca, dvitīya śrutaskandhagata saiṃtīsa, sthānāṅgasūtra-gata solahaaura vyavahārasūtra-gata cāra| ye saba milakara (5+37+16+4=62) vāsaṭha haiṃ| yadyapi ye sabhī pratimāeṃ cāritrasvarūpātmaka haiṃ, tathāpi ye viśiṣṭa śrutaśāliyoṃ ke hī hotī haiṃ, ataḥ śruta kī pradhānatā se inheṃ śrutasamādhipratimā ke rūpa meṃ kahā gayā hai|

[153 saptanavatisthānaka-samavāya 436-maṃdarassaṇaṃ pavvayassa paccacchimillāo caramaṃtāo gothubhassaṇaṃ āvāsapavvayassa paccacchimille caramaṃte esa ṇaṃ sattāṇauijoyaṇasahassāiṃ abāhāe aṃtare paṇṇatte| evaṃ caudisiṃ pi|
mandara parvata ke paścimī caramānta bhāga se gostubha āvāsa-parvata kā paścimī caramānta bhāga sattānavai hajāra yojana antara vālā kahā gayā hai| isī prakāra cāroṃ hī diśāoṃ meṃ jānanā cāhiye|
vivecana-meru parvata ke paścimī bhāga se jambūdvīpa kā pūrvī bhāga pacapana hajāra yojana hai aura usase gostubha parvata kā paścimī bhāga viyālīsa hajāra yojana dūra hai| ata: cāroṃ āvāsa parvatoṃ kā sūtrokta sattānavai hajāra yojana kā antara siddha ho jātā hai|
437–aṭuṇḍaṃ kammapagaḍīṇaṃ sattāṇauī uttarapagaḍīo paṇṇattāo| āṭhoṃ karmoṃ kī uttara prakṛtiyāṃ sattānavai (5+9+2+28+4+42+2+5=97) kahī gaī haiṃ|
438-hariseṇe ṇaṃ rāyā cāuraṃtacakkavaṭṭī desūṇāī sattāṇauiṃ vāsasayāī agāramajhe vasittā muṃḍe bhavittā ṇaṃ agārāo aṇagāriyaṃ pavvaie| __ . cāturantacakravartī hariṣeṇa rājā kucha kama sattānavai sau (9700) varṣa agāravāsa meṃ rahakara muṃḍita ho agāra se anagāritā meṃ pravajita hue|
|| saptanavatisthānaka samavāya samāpta|
aṣṭānavatisthānaka-samavāya 439-naṃdaṇavaṇassa ṇaṃ uvarillāo caramaṃtāo, paṃḍuyavaṇassa heṭṭhille caramaṃte esa ṇaṃ aṭṭhāṇauijoyaṇasahassāiṃ abāhāe aṃtare paṇṇatte|
naṃndanavana ke ūparī caramānta bhāga se pāṃḍuka vana ke nicale caramānta bhāga kā antara aṭṭhānavai hajāra yojana hai|
vivecana-nandanavana samabhūmi tala se pāṃca sau yojana ūṃcāī para avasthita hai aura usakī āṭhoṃ diśāoṃ meṃ avasthita kūṭa bhī pā~ca pā~ca sau yojana ūṃce haiṃ| ataḥ donoṃ milakara eka hajāra yojana ūṃcāī nandanavana kī ho jātī hai| meru kī ūṃcāī samabhūmi bhāga se ninyānavai hajāra yojana hai, usameṃ se ukta eka hajāra ke ghaṭā dene para sūtrokta aṭṭhānavai hajāra kā antara siddha ho jātā hai|
440-maṃdarassa ṇaṃ pavvayassa paccacchimillāo caramaṃtāo gothubhassa āvāsapavvayassa puracchimille caramaṃte esa ṇaṃ aṭṭhāṇaui joyaṇasahassāiṃ abāhāe aṃtare paṇṇatte| evaṃ caudisi pi|
mandara parvata ke paścimī caramāntabhāga se gostubha āvāsa parvata kā pūrvī caramānta bhāga aṭṭhānavai hajāra yojana antaravālā kahā gayā hai| isī prakāra cāroṃ hī diśāoṃ meṃ avasthita āvāsa parvatoṃ kā antara jānanā cāhie|

154]
[samavāyāṅgasūtra vivecana-sattānavaiveṃ sthāna ke sūtra meṃ pratipādita antara meṃ gostubha āvāsa-parvata ke eka hajāra yojana viṣkambha ko milā dene para aṭṭhānavai hajāra yojana kā antara siddha ho jātā hai|
441 -dāhiṇabharahassaṇaṃ dhaṇupiḍhe aṭṭhāṇaui joyaṇasayāī kiṃcūṇāī āyāmeṇaṃ paṇṇatte|
dakṣiṇa bharatakṣetra kā dhanu:pṛṣṭha kucha kama aṭṭhānavai sau (9800) yojana āyāma (lambāī) kī apekṣā kahā gayā hai|
442-uttarāo kaṭṭhāo sūrie paḍhamaṃ chammāsaṃ ayamāṇe egaṇapannāsatime maṃḍalagate aṭṭhāṇaui ekasaṭṭhibhāge muhattassa divasaṃkhettassa nivuḍḍhettā rayaṇikhettassa abhinivuḍḍittā ṇaṃ sūrie cāraṃ carai| dakkhiṇao ṇaṃ kaṭṭhāo sūrie doccaṃ chammāsaṃ ayamāṇe egūṇapannāsaime maṃḍalagate aṭṭhāṇaui ekasaṭṭhibhāe muhuttassa rayaṇikhittassa nivuḍḍhettā divasakhettassa abhinivuḍḍettā ṇaṃ sūrie cāraṃ carai|
uttara diśā se sarya prathama chaha māsa dakṣiṇa kī ora ātā haā unapacāsaveṃ maṃḍala ke ūpara ākara muhūrta ke ikasaṭhiye aṭṭhānavai bhāga divasakṣetra (dina) ke ghaṭākara aura rajanīkṣetra (rāta) ke baḍha़ākara saṃcāra karatā hai| isī prakāra dakṣiṇa diśā se sūrya dūsare chaha māsa uttara kī ora jātā huā unapacāsaveṃ maṃḍala ke ūpara ākara muhūrta ke aṭṭhānavai ikasaṭha bhāga rajanīkṣetra (rāta) ke ghaṭākara aura divasakṣetra (dina) ke baḍha़ākara saṃcāra karatā hai|
vivecana-sarya ke eka eka maṃḍala meṃ saṃcāra karane para maharta ke ikasaṭha bhāgoṃ meṃ se do bhāga pramāṇa dina kī vṛddhi yā rāta kī hāni hotī hai| ataḥ unapacāsaveṃ maṃḍala meṃ sūrya ke saṃcāra karane para muhūrta ke aṭṭhānavai ikasaṭha bhāga kī vṛddhi aura hāni siddha ho jātī hai| sūrya cāhe uttara se dakṣiṇa kī ora saṃcāra kare aura cāhe dakṣiṇa se uttara diśā kī ora saṃcāra kare, parantu unapacāsaveṃ maṃḍala para paribhramaṇa ke samaya dina yā rāta kī ukta vṛddhi yā hāni hī rahegī|
443-revaī-paḍhamajedrāpajavasāṇāṇaṃ egaṇavīsāe nakkhattāṇaṃ adrāṇauḍa tārāo tāraggeṇaṃ paṇṇattāo|
revatī se lekara jyeṣṭhā taka ke unnīsa nakṣatroṃ ke tāre aṭṭhānavai haiṃ|
vivecana- jyotiṣaśāstra ke anusāra revatī nakṣatra battīsa tārāvālā hai, aśvinī tīna tārā vālā hai, bharaṇī tīna tārāvālā hai, kṛttikā chaha tārāvālā hai, rohiṇī pā~ca tārāvālā hai, mṛgaśira tīna tārāvālā hai, ārdrā eka tārāvālā hai, punarvasu pā~ca tārāvālā hai, puṣya tīna tārāvālā hai, aśleṣā chaha tārāvālā hai, maghā sāta tārāvālā hai, pūrvāphālgunī do tārāvālā hai, uttarāphālgunī do tārāvālā hai, hasta pā~ca tārāvālā hai, citrā eka tārāvālā hai, svāti eka tārāvālā hai, viśākhā eka tārāvālā hai, anurādhā cāra tārāvālā hai, aura jyeṣṭhā nakṣatra tīna tārāvālā hai| ina unnīsoṃ nakṣatroṃ ke tārāoṃ ko joḍa़ne para (32+3+3+6+5+ 3+1+5+3+ 6+7+2+2+5+1+1+5+4+3=97) anya granthoṃ ke anusāra sattānavai saṃkhyā hī hotī hai| kintu prastuta sūtra meṃ unnīsa nakṣatroṃ ke tārāoṃ kī saṃkhyā aṭṭhānave (98) batāī gaī hai, ataḥ ukta nakṣatroṃ meṃ se kisī eka nakṣatra ke tārāoṃ kī saṃkhyā eka adhika honī cāhie| tabhī sūtrokta aṭṭhānavai saṃkhyā siddha hogī, aisā ṭīkākāra kā abhiprāya hai|
|| aṣṭānavatisthānaka samavāya samāpta ||

navanavatisthānaka-samavāya]
[155 navanavatisthānaka-samavāya 444-maṃdare ṇaṃ pavvae ṇavaṇauijoyaṇasahassāiṃ uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ paṇṇatte| naṃdaṇavaṇassa ṇaṃ puracchimillāo caramaṃtāo paccacchimille caramaṃte esa ṇaṃ navanauijoyaṇasayāiṃ abāhāe aṃtare paṇṇatte| evaṃ dakkhiṇillāo caramaṃtāo uttarille caramaṃte esa ṇaṃ ṇavaṇauijoyaṇasayāī abāhāe aṃtare paṇṇatte|
mandara parvata ninyānavai hajāra yojana ūṃcā kahā gayā hai| nandanavana ke pūrvī caramānta se paścimī caramānta ninyānavai sau (1900) yojana antaravālā kahā gayā hai| isī prakāra nandanavana ke dakṣiṇī caramānta se uttarī caramānta ninyānavai sau (9900) yojana antara vālā hai|
vivecana- meru parvata bhūtala para daśa hajāra yojana vistāravālā hai aura pā~ca sau yojana kī ūṃcāī para avasthita nandanavana ke sthāna para nau hajāra nau sau caupana yojana tathā eka yojana ke gyāraha bhāgoṃ meṃ se chaha bhāga-pramāṇa meru kā bāhya vistāra hai| aura bhītarī vistāra unyāsī sau caupana yojana aura eka yojana ke gyāraha bhāgoṃ meṃ se chaha bhāga-pramāṇa hai| pā~ca sau yojana nandanavana kī cauḍa़āī hai| isa prakāra meru kā ābhyantara vistāra aura donoṃ ora ke nandanavana kā pā~ca pā~ca sau yojana kā vistāra ye saba milakara prāyaḥ sūtrokta antara ho jātā hai|
445 - uttare paḍhame sūriyamaṃḍale navanauijoyaṇasahassāiṃ sāiregāiṃ āyāmavikkhaṃbheṇaṃ paṇṇatte|docce sūriyamaṃḍale navanauijoyaṇasahassāiṃ sāhiyāiṃ āyāmavikkhaṃbheṇaṃ paṇṇatte| taiyasūriyamaṃḍale navanauijoyaṇasahassāiṃ sāhiyāiṃ āyāmavikkhaṃbheṇaṃ paṇṇatte|
uttara diśā meṃ sūrya kā prathama maṃḍala āyāma-viṣkambha kī apekṣā kucha adhika ninyānavai hajāra yojana kahā gayā hai| dūsarā sūrya-maṃḍala bhī āyāma-viṣkambha kī apekṣā kucha adhika ninyānavai hajāra yojana kahā gayā hai| tīsarā sūryamaṃḍala bhī āyāma-viṣkambha kī apekṣā kucha adhika ninyānavai hajāra yojana kahā gayā hai|
vivecana-sūrya jisa ākāśa-mārga se meru ke cāroṃ ora paribhramaṇa karatā hai use sūrya-maṃḍala kahate haiṃ| jaba vaha uttara diśā ke sabase pahile maṃḍala para paribhramaṇa karatā hai, taba usa maṃḍala kī golākāra rūpa meṃ lambāī ninyānavai hajāra chaha sau cālīsa yojana (99640) hotī hai| jaba sūrya dūsare maṃḍala para paribhramaṇa karatā hai, taba usakī lambī ninyānavai hajāra chaha sau paiṃtālīsa yojana aura eka yojana ke ikasaṭha bhāgoṃ meṃ se paiṃtīsa bhāga-pramāṇa hotī hai| prathama maṃḍala se isa dūsare maṃḍala kī pāṃca yojana aura paiṃtīsa bhāga ikasaṭha vṛddhi kā kāraṇa yaha hai ki eka maṃḍala se dūsare maṃḍala kā antara do-do yojana kā hai tathā sūrya ke vimāna kā viṣkambha eka yojana ke ikasaṭha bhāgoṃ meṃ se aḍa़tālīsa bhāga-pramāṇa hai| ise dugunā kara dene para pā~ca yojana aura eka yojana ke ikasaṭha bhāgoṃ meṃ se paiṃtīsa bhāga-pramāṇa vṛddhi prathama

[samavāyāṅgasūtra maṃḍala se dūsare maṃḍala kī siddha ho jātī hai| isī prakāra dūsare maṃḍala ke viṣkambha meṃ 5 35 ke milā dene para (99645+5 35 =99651-9, ninyānavai hajāra chaha sau ikāvana yojana aura eka yojana ke ikasaṭha bhāgoṃ meṃ se nau bhāga-pramāṇa viṣkambha tīsare maṃḍala kā nikala ātā hai| ninyānavai hajāra meṃ ūpara jo prathama maṃḍala meṃ 640 yojana kī, dūsare maṃḍala meṃ 645 35/69 yojana kī aura tīsare maṃḍala meṃ 6519 yojana kī vṛddhi hotī hai, use sūtra meṃ 'sātireka' aura 'sādhika' pada se sūcita kiyā gayā hai, jisakā artha ninyānavai hajāra yojana se kucha adhika hotā hai|
446 –imīse ṇaṃ rayaṇappabhāe puḍhavīe aṃjaṇassa kaṃḍassa heṭṭhillāo caramaṃtāo vāṇamaṃtara-bhomejavihārāṇaṃ uvarimaṃte esa ṇaṃ navanaui joyaṇasayāī abāhāe aṃtare paṇṇatte|

vivecana-ratnaprabhā pṛthivī ke prathama kharakāṇḍa ke solaha kāṃḍoṃ meṃ aṃjanakāṃḍa daśavāṃ hai| usakā adhastana bhāga yahāṃ se daśa hajāra yojana dūra hai| prathama ratnakāṃḍa ke prathama sau yojanoṃ ke (bāda) vyantara devoṃ ke nagara haiṃ| ina sau ko daśa hajāra meṃ se (10,000-100=9900) ghaṭā dene para sūtrokta ninyānavai sau (9900) yojana kā antara siddha ho jātā hai|

[17 pāse ṇaṃ arahā purisādāṇīe ekkaṃ vāsasayaṃ savvāuyaṃ pālaittā siddhe buddhe jāva savvadukkhappahīṇe| evaṃ there vi ajjasuhamme| __ suvidhi puṣpadanta arhat sau dhanuṣa ūṃce the|
puruṣādānīya pārśva arhat eka sau varṣa kī samagra āyu bhoga kara siddha, buddha, karma-mukta, parinirvāṇa ko prāpta ho sarva duḥkhoṃ se rahita hue|

450-savve vi ṇaṃ dīhaveyaḍḍhapavvayā egamegaṃ gāuyasayaṃ urdū uccatteṇaṃ paṇṇattā| savvevi ṇaṃcullahimavaṃta-siharīvāsaharapavvayā egamegaṃjoyaṇasayaṃ uḍa़e uccatteṇaṃ paṇṇattā| egamegaṃ gāuyasayaṃ uvveheṇaṃ paṇṇattā| savve vi ṇaṃ kaṃcaṇagapavvayā egamegaṃ joyaṇasayaṃ uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ paṇṇattā| egamegaṃ gāuyasayaṃ uvveheṇaṃ paṇṇattā| egamegaṃ joyaṇasayaṃ mūle vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ paṇṇattā|
sabhī dīrgha vaitāḍhya parvata eka-eka sau gavyūti (kośa) ūṃce kahe gaye haiṃ| sabhī kṣullaka himavanta aura śikharī varṣadhara parvata eka-eka sau yojana ūṃce haiṃ| tathā ye sabhī varṣadhara parvata sau-sau gavyūti udvedha (bhūmi meṃ avagāha) vāle haiṃ| saṃbhī kāṃcanaka parvata eka-eka sau yojana ūṃce kahe gaye haiṃ| tathā ve sau-sau gavyūti udvedha vāle aura mūla meṃ eka-eka sau yojana viṣkambhavāle haiṃ|

anekottarikā-vṛddhi- samavāya]
[159 śramaṇa bhagavān mahāvīra ke saṃgha meṃ tīna sau caturdaśapūrvī muni the|
pā~ca sau dhanuṣa kī avagāhanāvāle caramaśarīrī siddhi ko prāpta puruṣoṃ (siddhoṃ) ke jīva pradeśoṃ kī avagāhanā kucha adhika tīna sau dhanuṣa kī hotī hai|
460-pāsassa ṇaṃ arahao purisādāṇīyassa adbhuṭṭhasayāiṃ coddasapuvvīṇaṃ saṃpayā hotyā| abhinaṃdaṇe ṇaṃ arahā achuṭṭāiṃdhaṇusayāī uḍḍhaṃ uccaseṇaṃ hotthā 350|
puruṣādānīya pārśva arhana ke sāḍha़e tīna sau caturdaśapūrviyoṃ kī sampadā thī| abhinandana arhan sāḍha़e tīna sau dhanuṣa ūṃce the|
461-saṃbhave ṇaṃ arahā cattāri dhaṇusayāī uḍḍhe uccatteṇaṃ hotthā| saṃbhava arhat cāra sau dhanuṣa ūṃce the|
462-savve vi ṇaṃ nisaḍhanīlavaṃtā vāsaharapavvayā cattāri-cattāri joyaṇasayāī uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ| paṇṇattā ||casāri cattārigāuyasayāī uvveheṇaṃ paṇṇattā| savve viṇaṃ vakkhārapavvayā 'ṇisaḍhanīlavaṃtavāsaharapavyayaṃteṇaṃ cattāri cattāri joyaṇasayāī uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ | cattāri cattāri gāuyasayāiṃ uvyeheṇaṃ paṇṇattā|
sabhī niṣadha aura nīlavanta varṣadhara parvata cāra-cāra sau yojana ūṃce tathā cāra-cāra sau gavyūti udvedha (gaharāī) vāle haiṃ| sabhī vakṣāra parvata niṣadha aura nīlavanta varṣadhara parvatoṃ ke samīpa cāra-cāra sau yojana ūṃce aura cāra-cāra. sau gavyūti udvedha vāle kahe gaye haiṃ|
463-āṇaya-pāṇaesu dosu kappesu cattāri vimāṇasayā paṇṇattā| ānata aura prāṇata ina do kalpoṃ meṃ donoṃ ke milākara cāra sau vimāna kahe gaye haiṃ|
464-samaṇassaṇaṃ bhagavao mahāvīrassa cattāri sayā vāīṇaṃ sadeva-maṇuyāsuraṃmi logaṃmi vāe aparājiyāṇaṃ ukkosiyā vāisaṃpayā hotthā 400|
śramaṇa bhagavān mahāvīra ke cāra sau aparājita vādiyoṃ kī utkṛṣṭa vādisampadā thī| ve vādī deva, manuṣya aura asuroṃ meṃ se bhī vāda meṃ parājita hone vāle nahīṃ the|
465-ajite ṇaṃ arahā addhapaṃcamāiṃ dhaṇusayāiṃ uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ hotthā| sagare ṇaṃ rāyā cāuraṃtacakkavaṭṭī addhapaṃcamāi dhaṇusayāiṃ uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ hotthā 450| ajita arhat sāḍha़e cāra sau dhanuṣa ūṃce the| cāturanta cakravartī sagara rājā bhī sāḍha़e cāra sau dhanuṣa ūṃce the|
466-savve vi ṇaṃ vakkhārapavvayā sīā-sīoāo mahānaīo maṃdarapavvayaṃte ṇaṃ paṃca paṃca joyaṇasayāī uḍḍaṃ uccatteṇaṃ paṃca paṃca gāuyasayāiṃ uvvaheṇaṃ paṇṇattāo| savve vi ṇaṃ vāsaharakūḍā paṃca paṃca joyaṇasayāī uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ hotthā| mūle paṃca paṃca joyaṇasayāī vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ paṇṇattā|
sabhī vakṣāra parvata sītā-sītodā mahānadiyoṃ ke aura mandara parvata ke samīpa pā~ca-pā~ca sau yojana

468–somaṇasa-gaṃdhamādaṇa-vijjuppabha-mālavaṃtāṇaṃ vakkhārapavvayāṇaṃ maṃdarapavvayaṃteṇaṃ paṃca paṃca joyaṇasayāī uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ, paṃca paṃca gāuyasayāiṃ uvveheṇaṃ paṇṇattā| savve viṇaṃ vakkhārapavvayakūḍā hari-harissahakūḍavajjā paṃca paṃca joyaṇasayāī uḍḍhe uccatteṇaṃ, mūle paṃca paṃca joyaṇasayāī āyāmavikkhaṃbheṇaṃ paṇṇattā| savve vi ṇaṃ ṇaṃdaṇakūḍā balakūḍavajjā paṃca paṃca joyaṇasayāī uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ, mūle paṃca paṃca joyaṇasayāiṃ āyāmavikkhaṃbheṇaṃ paṇṇattā|

470–saṇaṃkumāra-māhidesu kappesu vimāṇā chajoyaṇasayāī uḍḍhe uccatteṇaṃ paṇṇattā| cullahimavaṃtakūḍassa uvarillāo caramaṃtāo cullahimavaṃtassavāsaharapavvayassa samadharaṇitale esa ṇaṃ chajoyaṇasayāiṃ abāhāe aṃtare paṇṇatte| evaṃ siharīkūḍassa vi|
sanatkumāra aura māhendra kalpoṃ meṃ vimāna chaha sau yojana ūṃce kahe gaye haiṃ| kṣullaka himavanta kūṭa ke uparima caramānta se kṣullaka himavanta varṣadhara parvata kā samadharaṇītala chaha sau yojana antara vālā hai| isī prakāra śikharī kaṭa kā bhī antara jānanā cāhie|
vivecana–samabhūmi tala se kṣullaka himavanta aura śikharī varṣadhara parvata sau-sau yojana ūṃce haiṃ aura unake himakūṭa aura śikharī kūṭa pā~ca-pā~ca sau yojana ūṃce haiṃ, ata: ukta kūṭoṃ ke ūparī bhāga se ukta donoṃ hī varṣadhara parvatoṃ ke samabhūmi kā sūtrokta chaha-chaha sau yojana kā antara siddha ho jātā hai|
__ 471 -pāsassa ṇaṃ arahao chasayā vāīṇaṃ sadevamaṇuyāsure loe vāe aparājiāṇaṃ ukkosiyā vāisaṃpayā hotthā|abhicaṃde ṇaṃ kulagare chadhaṇusayāī uḍḍhe uccatteṇaṃ hotthā|vāsupujje ṇaṃ arihā chahiṃ purisasaehiṃ saddhiṃ muṃḍe bhavittā agārāo aṇagāriyaṃ pavvaie| 600|

472-baṃbha-laṃtaesu (dosu) kappesu vimāṇā satta satta joyaṇasayāī uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ paṇṇattā|
brahma aura lāntaka ina do kalpoṃ meṃ vimāna sāta-sāta sau yojana ūṃce kahe gaye haiṃ|
473-samaṇassa ṇaṃ bhagavao mahāvīrassa satta jiṇasayā hotthā| samaṇassa ṇaṃ bhagavao mahāvīrassa satta veuvviyasayā hotthā| ariṭṭhaṇemī ṇaṃ arahā satta vāsasayāī desūṇāī kevalapariyāgaṃ pāuṇittā siddhe buddhe jāva savvadukkhappahīṇe|
śramaṇa bhagavān mahāvīra ke saṃgha meṃ sāta sau kevalī the| śramaṇa bhagavān mahāvīra ke saṃgha meṃ sāta sau vaikriyalabdhidhārī sādhu the| ariṣṭanemi arhat kucha (54 dina) kama sāta sau varṣa kevaliparyāya meṃ raha kara siddha, buddha, karma-mukta, parinirvāṇa ko prāpta aura sarva duḥkhoṃ se rahita hue|
474-mahāhimavaṃtakūḍassa ṇaṃ uvarillāo caramaṃtāo mahāhimavaṃtassa vāsaharapavvayassa samadharaṇitale esa ṇaṃ satta joyaṇasayāī abāhāe aṃtare paṇṇatte| evaṃ ruppikūḍassa vi 700|
mahāhimavanta kūṭa ke ūparī caramānta bhāga se mahāhimavanta varṣadhara parvata kā samadharaṇītala sāta sau yojana antara vālā kahā gayā hai| isī prakāra rukmī kūṭa kā bhī antara jānanā cāhie|
vivecana–samabhūmi tala se mahāhimavanta aura rukmī varṣadhara parvata do-do sau yojana ūṃce haiṃ aura unake mahāhimavantakūṭa aura rukmīkūṭa pā~ca-pā~ca sau yojana ūṃce haiṃ| ata: ukta kūṭoṃ ke ūparī bhāga se ukta donoṃ hī varṣadhara parvatoṃ ke samabhūmi kā antara sāta-sāta sau yojana kā siddha ho jātā hai|
475–mahāsukka-sahassāresu dosu kappesu vimāṇā aṭṭhajoyaṇasayāī uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ paṇṇattā|
mahāśukra aura sahasrāra ina do kalpoṃ meṃ vimāna āṭha sau yojana ūṃce kahe gaye haiṃ|
476 - imīse ṇaṃ rayaṇappamāe [ puḍhavīe] paḍhame kaṃḍe aṭṭhasu joyaṇasaesu vāṇamaṃtarabhomejjavihārā paṇṇattā|
isa ratnaprabhā pṛthivī ke prathama kāṃḍa ke madhyavartī āṭha sau yojanoṃ meṃ vānavyantara bhaumeyaka devoṃ ke vihāra kahe gaye haiṃ|
vivecana-vanoṃ meṃ vṛkṣādi para utpanna hone se vyantaroṃ ko 'vāna' kahā jātā hai| tathā unake vihāra, nagara yā āvāsasthāna bhūminirmita haiṃ isalie unako 'bhaumeyaka' kahā jātā hai| daśaveṃ aṃjanakāṃḍa kā uparima bhāga samabhūmi bhāga se nau sau yojana nīce hai| usameṃ se prathama ratnakāṃḍa ke sau yojana kama kara dene para vānavyantaroṃ ke āvāsa aṃjanakāṃḍa ke uparima bhāga taka madhyavartī āṭha sau yojanoṃ meṃ pāye jāte haiṃ|
477-samaṇassa ṇaṃ bhagavao mahāvīrassa aṭṭhasayā aṇuttarovavāiyāṇaṃ devāṇaṃ

__ 478-imīse ṇaṃ rayaṇappabhāe puḍhavīe bahusamaramaṇijjāo bhūmibhāgāo aṭṭhahiṃ joyaṇasaehiṃ sūrie cāraṃ carati|

vivecana-samabhūmitala se niṣadha aura nīlavanta varṣadhara parvata cāra-cāra sau yojana ūṃce haiṃ aura unake niṣadha kūṭa aura nīlavanta kūṭa pā~ca-pā~ca sau yojana ūṃce haiṃ| ataḥ ukta kūṭoṃ ke ūparī bhāga se donoṃ hī varṣadhara parvatoṃ ke samabhūmi kā sūtrokta nau-nau sau yojana kā antara siddha ho jātā hai|
481-vimalavāhaṇe ṇaṃ kulagare ṇaṃ nava dhaṇusayāī uḍḍhe uccatteṇaṃ hotthā|

[ 163
niṣadha varṣadhara parvata ke uparima śikharatala se isa ratnaprabhā pṛthivī ke prathama kāṃḍa ke bahumadhya deśa bhāga kā antara nau sau yojana hai|
isī prakāra nīlavanta parvata kā bhī antara nau sau yojana kā samajhanā cāhie| varṣadhara parvatoṃ meṃ niṣadha parvata tīsarā ora nīlavanta parvata cauthā hai| donoṃ kā antara samāna hai |
483 - savve vi ṇaṃ gevejjavimāṇe dasa-dasa joyaṇasayāī uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ paṇṇatte |
savve vi ṇaṃ jamagapavvayā dasa-dasa joyaṇasayāī uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ paṇṇattā | dasa-dasa gāuyasayāī uvveṇaṃ paṇṇattā| mūle dasa-dasa joyaṇasayāiṃ āyāmavikkhabheṇaṃ paṇṇattā | evaṃ cittavicitta - kūḍā vi bhāṇiyavvā |
-
sabhī graiveyaka vimāna daśa daśa sau (1000) yojana ūṃce kahe gaye haiṃ |
sabhī yamaka parvata daśa daśa sau yojana ūṃce kahe gaye haiṃ| tathā daśa daśa sau gavyūti (1000 kośa) udvedha vāle kahe gaye haiṃ | ve mūla meṃ daśa - daśa sau yojana āyāma - viṣkambha vāle haiṃ| isī prakāra citra-vicitra kūṭa bhī kahanā cāhie |
vivecana - nīlavanta varṣadhara parvata ke uttara meṃ sītā mahānadī ke donoṃ kināroṃ para uttara kuru meṃ yamakaṃ nāma ke do parvata haiṃ| isī prakāra devakuru meṃ sītodā nadī ke donoṃ kināroṃ para niṣadha parvata ke dakṣiṇa meṃ citra-vicitra nāma ke do parvata haiṃ| ataḥ aḍha़āī dvīpa meṃ pā~ca-pā~ca sītā aura sītodā nadiyā~ haiṃ, ataḥ unake daśa-daśa yamaka kūṭoṃ kā nirdeśa prastuta sūtra meṃ kiyā gayā hai| ve sabhī eka-eka hajāra yojana ūṃce, eka-eka hajāra kośa bhūmi meṃ gahare aura golākāra hone se sarvatra eka-eka hajāra yojana āyāma-viṣkambha vāle arthāt cauḍa़e haiṃ|
484–savve vi ṇaṃ vaṭṭaveyaḍḍhapavvayā dasa-dasa joyaṇasayāī uḍḍhaṃ uccatteṇaṃ paṇṇattā| dasa-dasa gāuyasayāiṃ uvveheṇaṃ paṇṇattā | mūle dasa-dasa joyaṇasayāiṃ vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ paṇṇattā | sacvattha samā pallagasaṃṭhāṇasaṃṭhiyā paṇṇattā |
sabhī vṛtta vaitāḍhya parvata daśa daśa sau yojana ūṃce haiṃ| unakā udvedha daśa daśa sau gavyūti hai | ve mūla daśa daśa sau yojana viṣkambha vāle haiṃ| unakā ākāra ūpara-nīce sarvatra palyaṃka (ḍhola) ke samāna gola
hai |
485 - savve viṇaṃ hari-harissahakūḍā vakkhārakūḍavajjā dasa-dasa joyaṇasayāī uḍḍha uccatteṇaṃ paṇṇattā | mūle dasa joyaṇasayāī vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ (paṇṇattā ) | evaṃ balakūḍa vi naṃdaṇakūḍavajjā |
vakṣāra kūṭa ko choḍa़ kara sabhī hari aura harissaha kūṭa daśa daśa sau yojana ūṃce haiṃ aura mūla meṃ daśa sau yojana viṣkambha vāle haiṃ| isī prakāra nandana - kūṭa ko choḍa़ kara sabhī balakūṭa bhī daśa sau yojana vistāra vāle jānanā cāhie |
486 - arahā ṇaṃ ariṭṭhanemī dasa vāsasayāiṃ savvāuyaṃ pālaittā siddhe buddhe jāva savvadukkhappahī | pāsassa ṇaṃ arahao dasa sayāiṃ jiṇāṇaṃ hotthā | pāsassa ṇaṃ arahao dasa

[169 ___ ācāra saṃkṣepa se pā~ca prakāra kahā gayā hai, jaise-jñānācāra, darśanācāra cāritrācāra, tapācāra aura vīryācāra| isa pā~ca prakāra ke ācāra kā pratipādana karanevālā śāstra bhī ācāra kahalātā hai| ācārāṃga kī parimita sūtrārthapradāna rūpa vācanāeṃ haiṃ, saṃkhyāta upakrama ādi anuyogadvāra haiṃ, saṃkhyāta pratipattiyā~ haiṃ, saṃkhyāta veṣṭaka haiṃ, saṃkhyāta śloka haiṃ, aura saṃkhyāta niyuktiyā~ haiṃ|
vivecana-jñāna kā vinaya karanā, svādhyāya-kāla meṃ paṭhana-pāṭhana karanā, guru kā nāma nahīṃ chipānā, ādi āṭha prakāra ke vyavahāra ko jñānācāra kahate haiṃ| jina-bhāṣita tattvoṃ meṃ śaṃkā nahīṃ karanā, sāṃsārika sukhabhogoṃ kī ākāṃkṣā nahīṃ karanā, vicikitsā nahīṃ karanā ādi āṭha prakāra ke samyaktvī vyavahāra ke pālana karane ko darśanācāra kahate haiṃ| pā~ca mahāvratoṃ kā, pā~ca samitiyoṃ ādi rūpa cāritra kā nirdoṣa pālana karanā cāritrācāra hai| bahiraṃga aura antaraṃga tapoṃ kā sevana karanā tapācāra hai| apane āvaśyaka karttavyoṃ ke pālana karane meṃ śakti ko nahīṃ chipā kara yathāśakti unakā bhalī-bhāṃti se pālana karanā vīryācāra hai|
ukta pā~ca prakāra ke ācāra kī vācanāeṃ parīta (sīmita) haiṃ | ācārya-dvārā āgamasūtra aura sūtroṃ kā artha śiṣya ko denā 'vācanā' kahalātī hai| ācārāṅa kī aisī vācanāeṃ asaṃkhyāta ra hotī haiṃ, kintu parigaṇita hī hotī haiṃ, ataḥ unheṃ 'parīta' kahā gayā hai| ye vācanāeṃ utsarpiṇī aura avasarpiṇī kāla ke karmabhūmi ke samaya meṃ hī dī jātī haiṃ, akarmabhūmi yā bhogabhūmi ke yuga meṃ nahīṃ dī jātī
upakrama, naya, nikṣepa aura anugama ke dvārā vastu-svarūpa kā pratipādana kiyā jātā hai, ataeva unheṃ anuyoga-dvāra kahate haiṃ| ācārāṅga ke ye anuyogadvāra bhī saṃkhyāta hī haiṃ| vastu-svarūpa prajñāpaka vacanoṃ ko pratipatti kahate haiṃ | vibhinna mata vāloṃ ne padārthoṃ kā svarūpa bhinna-bhinna prakāra se mānā hai, aise matāntara bhī saṃkhyāta hī hote haiṃ| viśeṣa-eka viśeṣa prakāra ke chanda ko veḍha yā veṣṭaka kahate haiṃ matāntara se eka viṣaya kā pratipādana karane vālī śabdasaṃkalanā ko veḍha (veṣṭaka) kahate haiṃ| ācārāṅga ke aise chando viśeṣa bhī saṃkhyāta hī haiṃ| jisa chanda ke eka caraṇa yā pāda meṃ āṭha akṣara nibaddha hoṃ, aise cāra caraṇavāle anuṣṭapa chanda ko śloka kahate haiṃ| ācārāṅga meṃ ācāradharma ke pratipādana karane vāle śloka bhī saṃkhyāta hī haiṃ| sūtra-pratipādita saṃkṣipta artha ko śabda kī vyutpattipūrvaka yukti ke sātha pratipādana karanā niyukti kahalātī hai| aisī niyuktiyā~ bhī ācārāṅga kī saṃkhyāta hī haiṃ|
514-se ṇaṃ aṃgaṭhṭhayāe paḍhame aṃge, do suyakkhaṃdhā, paṇavīsaṃ ajjhayaṇā, paṃcāsīiṃ uddesaṇakālā, paṃcāsīiṃ samuddesaṇakālā, aṭṭhārasa padasahassāī, padaggeṇaṃ, saṃkhejjā akkharā, [aṇaṃtā gamā] aṇaṃtā pajjavā, parittā tasā, aṇaṃtā, thāvarā sāsayā kaḍā nibaddhā ṇikāiyā jiṇapaṇṇattā bhāvā āghavijaṃti paṇṇavinaṃti parūvijaṃti daṃsijjati nidaṃsijjati uvadaṃsijjati|

[samavāyāṅgasūtra uddeśanakāla haiṃ, [ikkīsa samuddeśanakāla haiṃ|] pada-gaṇanā kī apekṣā isameṃ bahattara hajāra pada haiṃ| saṃkhyāta akṣara haiṃ, ananta gama (jñāna-prakāra) haiṃ, ananta paryāya haiṃ, parīta trasa haiṃ| ananta sthāvara haiṃ| dravya-dṛṣṭi se sarva bhāva śāśvata haiṃ, paryāya-dṛṣṭi se anitya haiṃ, nibaddha haiṃ, nikācita (dṛḍha़ kiye gaye) haiṃ, jina-prajñapta haiṃ| ina saba bhāvoṃ kā isa aṃga meṃ kathana kiyā jātā hai, prajñāpana kiyā jātā hai, prarūpaṇa kiyā jātā hai, nidarśana kiyā jātā hai aura upadarśana kiyā jātā hai| isa aṃga kā adhyetā ātmā jñātā ho jātā hai, vijñātā ho jātā hai| isa prakāra caraṇa aura karaṇa prarūpaṇā ke dvārā vastu ke svarūpa kā kathana, prajñāpana, parūpaṇa, nidarśana aura upadarśana kiyā jātā hai| yaha tīsare sthānāṅga kā paricaya hai ||3||
522-se kiṃ taṃ samavāe? samavāe ṇaṃ sasamayā sūijjaṃti, parasamayā sūijjaṃti, sasamayaparasamayā sūijjaṃti| jīvā sūijjaṃta, ajīvā sūijjaṃti, jīvājīvā sūijjaṃti, loge sūijjati, aloge suijjati, logāloge sūijjati|
samavāyāṅga kyā hai? isameṃ kyā varṇana hai?
samavāyāṅga meṃ svasamaya sūcita kiye jāte haiṃ, parasamaya sūcita kiye jāte haiṃ aura svasamaya-parasamaya sūcita kiye jāte haiṃ| jīva sūcita kiye jāte haiṃ, ajīva sūcita kiye jāte haiṃ, aura jīva-ajīva sūcita kiye jāte haiṃ| loka sūcita kiyā jātā hai, aloka sūcita kiyā jātā hai aura loka aloka sūcita kiyā jātā hai|
523 -samavāeṇaṃ ekāiyāṇaṃ egaṭṭhāṇaṃ eguttariyaparivuḍḍhīe duvālasaṃgassa vi gaṇipiḍagassa pallavagge samaṇugāijjai, ṭhāṇagasayassa bārasavihavittharassa suyaṇāṇassa jagajīvahiyassa bhagavao samāseṇaṃ samoyāre āhijjati| tattha ya ṇāṇāvihappagārā jīvājīvā ya vaṇiyā, vitthareṇa avare vi ya bahuvihā visesā naraga-tiriya-maṇu-suragaṇāṇaṃ āhārussāsalesā-āvāsa-saṃkha-āyayappamāṇa-uvavāya-cavaṇa-uggahaṇovahi-veyaṇavihāṇa-upaoga-jogaiṃdiya-kasāyā vivihā ya jīvajoṇī vikkhaṃbhussehaparirayappamāṇaṃ vihivisesā ya maṃdarādīṇaṃ mahīdharāṇaṃ kulagara-titthagara-gaṇaharāṇaṃ sammatta-bharahāhivāṇacakkīṇaṃ ceva cakkahara-halaharāṇa ya vāsāṇa ya nigamā ya samāe ee aṇṇe ya evamāi ettha vitthareṇaṃ atthā samāhinjaṃti|
samavāyāṅga ke dvārā eka, do, tīna ko ādi lekara eka-eka sthāna kī parivṛddhi karate hue śata, sahasra aura koṭākoṭī taka ke kitane hī padārthoṃ kā aura dvādaśāṅga gaṇipiṭaka ke pallavānoṃ (paryāyoṃ ke pramāṇa) kā kathana kiyā jātā hai| sau taka ke sthānoṃ kā, tathā bāraha aṃgarūpa meṃ vistāra ko prāpta, jagat ke jīvoṃ ke hitakāraka bhagavān śrutajñāna kā saṃkṣepa se samavatāra kiyā jātā hai| isa samavāyāṅga meṃ nānā prakāra ke bheda-prabheda vāle jīva aura ajīva padārtha varṇita haiṃ| tathā vistāra se anya bhī bahuta prakāra ke viśeṣa tattvoṃ kā, naraka, tiryaṃca, manuṣya aura deva gaṇoṃ ke āhāra, ucchvāsa,leśyā, āvāsa-saṃkhyā, unake āyāmaviṣkambha kā pramāṇa, upapāta (janma) cyavana (maraṇa) avagāhanā, upadhi, vedanā, vidhāna (bheda), upayoga, yoga, indriya, kaṣāya, nānā prakāra kī jīva-yoniyā~, parvata-kūṭa ādi ke viṣkambha (cauḍa़āī) utsedha (ūṃcāī) pariraya (paridhi) ke pramāṇa, mandara ādi mahīdharoṃ (parvatoṃ) ke vidhi-(bheda) viśeṣa, kulakaroṃ,

525-se ṇaṃ aṃgaṭṭhayāe cautthe aṃge, ege ajjhayaṇe, ege suyakkhaṃdhe, ege uddesaṇakāle (ege samuddesaṇakāle)| cauyāle padasayasahasse padaggeṇaṃ paṇṇatte|saṃkhejāṇi akkharāṇi, aṃṇatā gamā, aṇaṃtā pajjavā, parittā tasā, aṇaṃtā thāvarā sāsayā kaḍā nibaddhā nikāiyā jiṇapaṇṇattā bhāvā āghavijaṃti paṇṇavinjati parūvijaṃti nidaṃsirjati uvadaṃsijaṃti| se evaṃ āyā, evaṃ viṇṇāyā, evaṃ caraṇa-karaṇa parūvaṇayā āghavijjaṃti.| settaṃ samavāe 4|

526 –se kiṃ taṃ vivāhe? vivāheṇa sasamayā viāhijjaṃti, parasamayā viāhijjaṃti, sasamaya-parasamayā viāhijjaṃti, jīvā viāhijaṃti, ajīvā viāhijjati, jīvājīvā viāhijjaṃti, loge viāhijjai, aloe viāhijjai, logāloge viāhijjai|
vyākhyāprajñapti kyā hai - isameṃ kyā varṇana hai?

[177 paraloiaiḍḍīvisesā 10, bhoyapariccāyā pavvajjāo suyapariggahā tavovahāṇāiṃ pariyāgā 15, saṃlehaṇāo bhattapaccakkhāṇāī pāovagamaṇāī devalogagamaṇāī sukulapaccāyāyāiṃ 20, puṇabohilābhā aṃtakiriyāo 22 ya āghavinaṃti parūvijjaṃti daṃsijati nidaṃsijjaṃti uvadaṃsijjaṃti|

531 – nāyādhammakahāsu ṇaṃ pavvaiyāṇaṃ viṇaya-karaṇa-jiṇasāmisāsaṇavare saṃjamapaiṇṇapālaṇadhii-mai-vavasāyadubbalāṇaṃ 1, tavaniyama-tavovahāṇa-raṇa-duddhara-bhara-bhaggāṇisahaya-ṇisiṭṭhāṇaṃ 2, ghora-parīsaha-parājiyāṇaṃ'sahapāraddha-ruddhasiddhālaya-mahaggā-niggayāṇaṃ 3, visayasuha-tuccha-āsāvasa-dosamucchiyāṇaṃ 4, virāhiya-caritta-nāṇa-daṃsaṇa-aiguṇavivihappayāra-nissārasunnayāṇaṃ5, saṃsāra-apāra-dukkhaduggai-bhavaviviha-paraṃparāpavaṃcā 6.dhīrāṇa ya jiyaparisaha-kasāya-seṇṇa-dhii-dhaṇiya-saṃjama-ucchāha-nicchiyāṇaṃ 7, ārāhiyanāṇa-daṃsaṇacarittajoga-nissalla-suddhasiddhālaya-maggamabhimuhāṇaṃ surabhavaṇa-vimāṇasukkhāiṃ aṇovamāiṃ bhuttūṇa ciraṃ ca bhogabhogāṇi tāṇi divvāṇi maharihāṇi| tato ya kālakkamacuyāṇa jaha ya puṇo laddhasiddhimaggāṇaṃ aṃtakiriyā| caliyāṇa ya sadeva-māṇussadhīra-karaṇa-kāraṇāṇi bodhaṇaaṇusāsaṇāṇi guṇa-dosa darisaṇāṇi| diḻeṃte paccaye ya soūṇa logamuṇiṇo jaha ya ṭhiyāsāsaṇammi jara-maraṇa-nāsaṇakare ārāhiasaṃjamā ya suralogapaḍiniyattā oventi jaha sāsayaṃ sivaṃ savvadukkhamokkhaṃ, ee aṇṇe ya evamāiatthā vitthareṇa ya|
jñātādharmakathā meṃ pravrajita puruṣoṃ ke vinaya-karaṇa-pradhāna, pravara jina-bhagavān ke śāsana kī saṃyama-pratijñā ke pālana karane meṃ jinakī dhṛti (dhīratā), mati (buddhi) aura vyavasāya (puruṣārtha) durbala hai, tapaścaraṇa kā niyama aura tapa kā paripālana karane rūpa raṇa (yuddha) ke durdhara bhāra ko vahana karane se bhagna haiṃ - parāṅmukha ho gaye haiṃ, ataeva atyanta aśakta hokara saṃyama-pālana karane kā saṃkalpa choḍa़kara baiṭha gaye haiṃ, ghora parīṣahoṃ se parājita ho cuke haiṃ isalie saṃyama ke sātha prārambha kiye gaye mokṣa-mārga ke avaruddha ho jāne se jo siddhālaya ke kāraṇabhūta mahāmūlya jñānādi se patita haiṃ, jo indriyoṃ ke tuccha viṣaya-sukhoṃ kī āśā ke vaśa hokara rāgādi doṣoṃ se mūrcchita ho rahe haiṃ, cāritra, jñāna, darśana svarūpa yati-guṇoṃ se aura unake vividha prakāroṃ ke abhāva se jo sarvathā niḥsāra aura śūnya haiṃ, jo saṃsāra ke apāra duḥkhoṃ kī aura naraka, tiryaṃcādi nānā durgatiyoṃ kī bhava-paramparā ke prapaṃca meṃ paḍa़e hue haiṃ, aise patita puruṣoṃ kī kathāeṃ haiṃ| tathā jo dhīra vīra haiṃ, parīṣahoṃ aura kaṣāyoṃ kī senā ko jītane vāle haiṃ, dhairya ke dhanī haiṃ,saṃyama meṃ utsāha

[samavāyāṅgasūtra rakhane aura bala-vīrya ke prakaṭa karane meṃ dṛḍha़ niścaya vāle haiṃ, jñāna, darśana, cāritra aura samādhi-yoga kī jo ārādhanā karane vāle haiṃ, mithyādarśana, māyā aura nidānādi śalyoṃ se rahita hokara śuddha nirdoṣa siddhālaya ke mārga kī ora abhimukha haiṃ, aise mahāpuruṣoṃ kī kathāeṃ isa aṃga meṃ kahī gaī haiṃ| tathā jo saṃyamaparipālana kara devaloka meṃ utpanna ho deva-bhavanoṃ aura deva-vimānoṃ ke anupama sukhoṃ ko aura divya, mahāmūlya, uttama, bhoga-upabhogoṃ ko cira-kāla taka bhoga kara kālakrama ke anusāra vahā~ se cyuta ho punaḥ yathāyogya mukti ke mārga ko prāpta kara antakriyā se samādhimaraṇa ke samaya karma-vaśa vicalita ho gaye haiṃ, unako devoṃ aura manuṣyoṃ ke dvārā dhairya dhāraṇa karāne meṃ kāraṇabhūta, saṃbodhanoṃ aura anuśāsanoṃ ko, saṃyama ke guṇa aura saṃyama se patita hone ke doṣa-darśaka dṛṣṭāntoṃ ko, tathā pratyayoṃ ko, arthān bodhi ke kāraṇabhūta
ko sanakara śakaparivrājaka ādi laukika mani jana bhī jarā-maraṇa kā nāśa karane vāle jinaśāsana meṃ jisa prakāra se sthita hue, unhoṃne jisa prakāra se saṃyama kī ārādhanā kī, punaḥ devaloka meṃ utpanna hae, vahā~ se ākara manuṣya ho jisa prakāra śāśvata sakha ko aura sarvadaḥkha-vimokṣa ko prāpta kiyā. unakī tathā isī prakāra ke anya aneka mahāpuruṣoṃ kī kathāeṃ isa aṃga meṃ vistāra se kahī gaī haiṃ|
532–ṇāyādhammakahāsu ṇaṃ parittā vāyaṇā, saṃkhejjā aṇuogadārā, saṃkhenjāo paḍivattīo, saṃkhejjā veḍhā, saṃkhejjā silogā, saṃkhejjāo ninjuttīo, saṃkhejjāo saṃgahaṇīo|

yaha jñātādharmakathā aṃgarūpa se chaṭhā aṃga hai| isameṃ do śrutaskandha haiṃ unameṃ se prathama śrutaskandha ( jñāta) ke unnīsa adhyayana haiṃ| ve saṃkṣepa se do prakāra ke kahe gaye haiṃ – carita aura kalpita | (inameṃ se ādi ke dasa adhyayanoṃ meṃ ākhyāyikā ādirūpa avāntara bheda nahīṃ hai| śeṣa nau adhyayanoṃ meṃ se pratyeka meṃ 540 ākhyāyikāeṃ haiṃ| pratyeka ākhyāyikā meṃ 500 upākhyāyikāeṃ aura pratyeka upākhyāyikā meṃ 500 ākhyāyikā upākhyāyikāeṃ haiṃ| ina kā kula joḍa (540 x 500 x 500 x 9 = 1215000000) eka sau ikkīsa karoḍa़ pacāsa lākha hotā hai|

___ 542-se kiṃ taṃ aṇuttarovavāiyadasāo ? aṇuttarovavāiyadasāsu ṇaṃ aṇuttarovavāiyāṇaṃ nagarāiṃ ujjāṇāiṃ ceiyāiṃ vaṇakhaṃḍā rāyāṇo ammā-piyaro samosaraṇāī dhammāyariyā dhammakahāo ihaloga-paraloga-iḍḍivisesā bhogapariccāyā pavvajjāo suyapariggahā tavovahaṇāiṃ pariyāgo paḍimāo saṃlehaṇāo bhattapāṇapaccakkhāṇāiṃ pāovagamaṇāī aṇuttarovavavāo sukulapaccāyāi, puṇo bohilābho aṃtakiriyāo ya āghavijaṃti parūvijaṃti daṃsijaṃti nidaṃsijjaṃti uvadaṃsijati|
anuttaropapātikadaśā kyā hai ? isameṃ kyā varṇana hai ?
anuttaropapātikadaśā meṃ anuttara vimānoṃ se utpanna hone vāle mahā anagāroṃ ke nagara, udyāna caitya, vanakhaṃḍa, rājagaṇa, mātā-pitā, samavasaraṇa, dharmācārya, dharmakathāeṃ, ihalaukika pāralaukika viśiṣṭa ṛddhiyāṃ, bhoga-parityāga, pravrajyā, śruta kā parigrahaṇa, tapa-upadhāna, paryāya, pratimā, saṃlekhanā, bhakta-pratyākhyāna, pādapopagamana, anuttara vimānoṃ meṃ utpāda, phira sukula meṃ janma, puna: bodhi-lābha aura antakriyāeṃ kahī gaī haiṃ,unakī prarūpaṇā kī gaī hai, unakā darśana, nidarśana aura upadarśana karāyā gayā hai|
543– aṇuttarovavāiyadasāsu ṇaṃ titthakarasamosaraṇāiṃ paramamaṃgalla-jagahiyāṇi jiṇātisesā ya bahuvisesā jiṇasīsāṇaṃ ceva samaṇagaṇa-pavara-gaṃdhahatthīṇaṃ thirajasāṇaṃ parīsahaseṇṇa-riubala-pamaddaṇāṇaṃ tava ditta-caritta-ṇāṇa-sammatta sāra-vivihappagāra-vittharapasatthaguṇasaṃjuyāṇaṃ aṇagāramahariṇīṇaṃ aṇagāraguṇāṇa vaṇṇao, uttamavaratava-visiṭṭhaṇāṇajogajuttāṇaṃ,jaha ya jagahiyaṃ bhagavao jārisā iḍiṭvisesā devāsura-māṇusāṇaṃ parisāṇaṃ pāubbhāvā ya jiṇasamīvaṃ, jaha ya uvāsaṃti, jiṇavaraṃ jaha ya parikahaṃti dhammaṃ logaguru amara-nara-sura-gaṇāṇaṃ soūṇa ya tassa bhāsiyaṃ avasesakammavisayavirattā narā jahā abbhuti dhammamurālaṃ saṃjamaṃ tavaṃ cāvi bahuvihappagāraṃ jaha bahūṇi vāsāṇi aṇucarittā ārāhiyanāṇa daṃsaṇa-caritta-jogā jiṇavayaṇamaṇugayamahiyaṃ bhāsiyā jiṇavarāṇa hiyayeṇamaṇuṇṇettā je ya jahiṃ jattiyāṇi bhattāṇi cheaittā labhrūṇa ya samāhimuttamajjhāṇajogajuttā uvavannā muṇivarottamā jaha aṇuttaresu pāvaṃti jaha aṇuttaraṃ tatthaṃ visayasokkhaṃ| tao ya cuā kameṇa kāhiṃti saṃjayā jahā ya aṃtakiriyaṃ ee anne ya evamāiatthā vitthareṇa|
anuttaropapātikadaśā meṃ parama maṃgalakārī, jagat-hitakārī tīrthaṃkaroṃ ke samavasaraṇa aura bahuta prakāra ke jina-atiśayoṃ kā varṇana hai| tathā jo śramaṇajanoṃ meṃ, varagandhahastī ke samāna śreṣṭha haiṃ, sthira yaśavāle haiṃ, parīṣaha-senā rūpī śatru-bala ke mardana karane vāle haiṃ, tapa se dīpta haiṃ, jo cāritra, jñāna, samyaktvarūpa sāravāle aneka prakāra ke viśāla praśasta guṇoṃ se saṃyukta haiṃ, aise anagāra maharṣiyoṃ ke anagāra-guṇoṃ kā anuttaropapātikadaśā meṃ varṇana hai| atīva, śreṣṭha tapoviśeṣa se aura viśiṣṭa jñāna-yoga se yukta haiṃ, jinhoṃne jagat hitakārī bhagavān tīrthaṃkaroṃ kī jaisī parama āścaryakāriṇī ṛddhiyoṃ kī viśeṣatāoṃ

dvādaśāṃga gaṇi-piṭaka ]
ko aura deva, asura, manuṣyoṃ kī sabhāoṃ ke prādurbhāva ko dekhā hai, ve mahāpuruṣa jisa prakāra jinavara ke samīpa jākara unakī jisa prakāra se upāsanā karate haiṃ tathā asura, nara, suragaṇoṃ ke lokaguru ve jinavara jisa prakāra se unako dharma kā upadeśa dete haiṃ ve kṣīṇakarmā mahāpuruṣa unake dvārā upadiṣṭa dharma ko sunakara ke apane samasta kāma-bhogoṃ se aura indriyoṃ ke viṣayoṃ se virakta hokara jisa prakāra se udāra dharma ko aura vividha prakāra se saṃyama aura tapa ko svīkāra karate haiṃ, tathā jisa prakāra se bahuta varṣoṃ taka unakā ācaraṇa karake, jñāna, darśana, cāritra yoga kī ārādhanā kara jina-vacana ke anugata (anukūla) pūjita dharma kā dūsare bhavya jīvoṃ ko udeśa dekara aura apane śiṣyoṃ ko adhyayana karavā tathā jinavaroṃ kī hṛdaya se ārādhanā kara ve uttama munivara jahāṃ para jitane bhaktoṃ kā anaśana ke dvārā chedana kara, samādhi ko prāpta kara aura uttama dhyāna yoga se yukta hote huye jisa prakāra se anuttara vimānoṃ meṃ utpanna hote haiṃ aura vahā~ jaise anupama viṣaya - saukhya ko bhogate haiṃ, usa saba kā anuttaropapātikadaśā meṃ varṇana kiyā gayā hai | tatpaścāt vahā~ se cyuta hokara ve jisa prakāra se saṃyama ko dhāraṇa kara antakriyā kareṃge aura mokṣa ko prāpta kareṃge, ina saba kā tathā isī prakāra ke anya arthoṃ kā vistāra se isa aṃga meṃ varṇana kiyā gayā hai|
544 - aṇuttarovavāiyadasāsu ṇaṃ parittā vāyaṇā, saṃkhejjā aṇuogadārā, saṃkhe paḍivattīo, saṃkhejjā veḍhā, saṃkhejjā silogā, saṃkhejjāo nijjuttīo saṃkhejjāo saṃgahaṇīo|
anuttaropapātikadaśā meṃ parīta vācanāeṃ haiṃ, saṃkhyāta anuyogadvāra haiṃ, saṃkhyāta pratipattiyāṃ haiṃ, saṃkhyāta veḍha haiṃ, saṃkhyāta śloka haiṃ, saṃkhyāta niyuktiyāṃ haiṃ aura saṃkhyāta saṃgrahaṇiyāṃ haiṃ |
545 - se ṇaṃ aṃgaṭṭayāe navame aṃge, ege suyakkhaṃdhe, dasa ajjhayaṇā, tinni vaggā, dasa uddesaṇakālā, dasa samuddesaṇakālā, saṃkhejjāiṃ payasayasahassāiṃ payaggeṇaṃ paṇṇattāiṃ | saṃkhejjāṇi akkharāṇi, anaṃtā gamā, aṇaṃtā pajjavā, parittā tasā, aṇaṃtā thāvarā, sāsayā kaḍā ṇibaddha ṇikāiyā jiṇapaṇṇattā bhāvā āghavijjaṃti paṇṇavijjaṃti parūvijjaṃti nidaṃsijjaṃti uvadaṃsijrjjati | se evaṃ āyā, se evaṃ ṇāyā evaṃ viṇṇāyā, evaṃ caraṇa-karaṇaparūvaṇayā āghavijjaṃti0 | se taṃ aṇuttarovavāiyadasāo 9 |
yaha anuttaropapātikadaśā aṃgarūpa se nauvāṃ aṃga hai| isameṃ eka śrutaskandha hai, daśa adhyayana haiṃ, tīna varga haiṃ, daśa uddeśana - kāla haiṃ, daśa samuddeśana - kāla haiṃ tathā pada-gaṇanā kī apekṣā saṃkhyāta lākha pada kahe gaye haiṃ| isameṃ saṃkhyāta akṣara haiṃ, ananta gama haiṃ, ananta paryāya haiṃ, parimita trasa haiṃ, ananta sthāvara haiṃ| ye saba śāśvata, kṛta, nibaddha nikācita, jina-prajñapta bhāva isa aṃga meṃ kahe jāte haiṃ, prajñāpita kiye jāte haiṃ, prarūpita kiye jāte haiṃ, nidarśita kiye jāte haiṃ, aura upadarśita kiye jāte haiṃ| isa aṃga ke dvārā ātmā jñātā hotā hai, vijñātā hotā
hai
isa prakāra caraṇa aura karaṇa kī prarūpaṇā ke dvārā vastu-svarūpa kā kathana, prajñāpana, prarūpaṇa, nidarśana aura upadarśana kiyā jātā hai| yaha nauveṃ anuttaropapātikadaśā aṃga kā paricaya hai |
546 – se kiṃ taṃ paṇhāvāgaraṇāṇi ? paṇhāvāgaraṇesu aṭṭuttaraṃ pasaṇasayaṃ aṭṭhattaraṃ apasiṇasayaṃ aṭṭhattaraṃ pasiṇāpasiṇasayaṃ vijjāisayā nāga - suvannehiṃ saddhiṃ divvā saṃvāyā āghavijjati |
1.
ṭīkākāra kā kathana hai-varga adhyayanoṃ kā samūha kahalātā hai| varga meṃ adhyayana dasa haiṃ aura eka varga kā uddeśana eka sātha hotā hai | ataeva isake uddeśanakāla tīna hī hone cāhie| nandīsūtra meṃ bhī tīna kā hī ullekha hai| kintu yahā~ daśa uddeśanakāla kahane kā abhiprāya kyā , samajha meṃ nahīṃ ātā| - sampādaka

184]
[samavāyāṅgasūtra praśnavyākaraṇa aṃga kyā hai-isameṃ kyā varṇana hai?
praśnavyākaraṇa aṃga meṃ eka sau āṭha praśnoṃ, eka sau āṭha apraśnoṃ aura eka sau āṭha praśnāpraśnoṃ ko, vidyāoṃ ke atiśayoṃ ko tathā nāgoṃ-suparṇoṃ ke sātha divya saṃvādoṃ ko kahā gayā hai|
vivecana-aṃguṣṭhapraśna ādi maṃtravidyāeṃ praśna kahalātī haiṃ | jo vidyāeṃ jijñāsu ke dvārā pūche jāne para śubhāśubha phala batalātī haiṃ, ve praśna-vidyāeṃ kahalātī haiṃ| jo vidyāeṃ maṃtra-vidhi se jāpa kiye jāne para binā pūche hī śubhāśubha phala ko kahatī haiṃ, ve apraśna-vidyāeṃ kahalātī haiṃ| tathā jo vidyāeṃ kucha praśnoṃ ke pūche jāne para aura kucha ke nahīṃ pūche jāne para bhī śubhāśubha phala ko kahatī haiṃ, ve praśnāpraśna vidyāeṃ kahalātī haiṃ| ina tīnoṃ prakāra kī vidyāoṃ kā praśnavyākaraṇa aṃga meṃ varṇana kiyā gayā hai| tathā staṃbhana. vaśīkaraṇa. uccāṭana ādi vidyāeṃ vidyātiśaya kahalātī haiṃ| evaṃ vidyāoṃ ke sādhanakāla meṃ nāgakumāra, suparṇakumāra tathā yakṣādikoṃ ke sātha sādhaka kā jo divya tāttvika vārtālāpa hotā hai vaha divyasaṃvāda kahā gayā hai| ina sabakā isa aṃga meṃ nirūpaṇa kiyā gayā hai|
547-paṇhāvāgaraṇadasāsu ṇaṃ sasamaya-parasamaya paṇṇavaya-patteabuddha-vivihatthabhāsābhāsiyāṇaṃ aisayaguṇa-uvasama-ṇāṇappagāra-āyariyabhāsiyāṇaṃ vitthareṇaṃ viramahesīhiṃ vivihavittharabhāsiyāṇaṃca jagahiyāṇaṃ adāgaṃguṭu-bāhu-asi-maṇi-khoma-aicca bhāsiyāṇaṃ vivihamahāpasiṇavijjā-maṇapasiṇa-vijjā-devayapayoga-pahāṇa-guṇappagāsiyāṇaṃ sabbhūyaduguṇappabhāvanaragaṇamaivimhayakarāṇaṃ aisayamaīyakāla-samaya-dama-sama-titthakaruttamasma ṭhiikaraṇakāraṇāṇaṃ durahigama-duravagāhassa savvasavvannusammaassa abuhajaṇa-vibohaṇakarassa paccakkhayapaccayakarāṇaṃ paṇhāṇaṃ vivihaguṇamahatthā jiṇavarappaṇīyā āghavinaṃti|
praśnavyākaraṇadaśā meṃ svasamaya-parasamaya ke prajñāpaka pratyekabuddhoṃ ke vividha arthoṃ vālī bhāṣāoṃ dvārā kathita vacanoṃ kā, āmā~ṣadhi ādi atiśayoṃ, jñānādi guṇoṃ aura upaśama bhāva ke pratipādaka nānā prakāra ke ācāryabhāṣitoṃ kā. vistāra se kahe gaye vīra maharṣiyoṃ ke jagata hitakārī aneka prakāra ke vistṛta subhāṣitoṃ kā, ādarśa (darpaṇa) aṃguṣṭha, bāhu, asi, maṇi, kṣauma (vastra) aura sūrya ādi ke āśraya se diye gaye vidyā-devatāoṃ ke uttaroṃ kā isa aṃga meṃ varṇana hai| aneka mahāpraśnavidyāeṃ vacana se hī praśna karane para uttara detī haiṃ, aneka vidyāeṃ mana se cintita praśnoṃ kā uttara detī haiṃ, aneka vidyāeṃ aneka adhiṣṭhātā devatāoṃ ke prayoga-viśeṣa kī pradhānatā se aneka arthoṃ ke saṃvādaka guṇoṃ ko prakāśita karatī haiṃ aura apane sadbhūta (vāstavika) dviguṇa prabhāvaka uttaroṃ ke dvārā jana samudāya ko vismita karatī haiṃ| una vidyāoṃ ke camatkāroṃ aura satya vacanoṃ se logoṃ ke hṛdayoṃ meṃ yaha dṛḍha़ viśvāsa utpanna hotā hai ki atīta kāla ke samaya meṃ dama aura śama ke dhāraka, anya matoṃ ke śāstāoṃ se viśiṣṭa jina tīrthaṃkara hue haiṃ aura ve yathārthavādī the, anyathā isa prakāra ke satya vidyāmaṃtra saṃbhava nahīṃ the, isa prakāra saṃśayaśīla manuṣyoṃ ke sthirīkaraṇa ke kāraṇabhūta durabhigama (gambhīra) aura duravagāha (kaṭhinatā se avagāhana-karane ke yogya) sabhī sarvajñoṃ ke dvārā sammata, abudha (ajña) janoṃ ko prabodha karane vāle, pratyakṣa pratīti-kāraka praśnoṃ ke vividha guṇa aura mahān artha vāle jinavara-praṇīta uttara isa aṃga meṃ kahe jāte haiṃ, prajñāpita kiye jāte haiṃ, prarūpita kiye jāte haiṃ, nidarśita kiye jāte haiṃ aura upadarśita kiye jāte haiṃ|

[185 548-paṇhāvāgaraṇesu ṇaṃ parittā vāyaṇā, saṃkhejjā aṇuogadārā, saṃkhenjāo paḍivattīo, saṃkhejjā veḍhā, saṃkhejjā silogā, saṃkhenjāo nijjuttīo, saṃkhejjāo saṃgahaṇīo|
praśnavyākaraṇa aṃga meṃ parīta vācanāeṃ haiṃ, saṃkhyāta anuyogadvāra haiṃ, saṃkhyātta pratipattiyā~ haiṃ, saṃkhyāta veḍha haiṃ, saṃkhyāta śloka haiṃ, saṃkhyāta niyuktiyāṃ haiṃ aura saṃkhyāta saṃgrahaṇiyāṃ haiṃ|
549-se ṇaṃ aṃgaṭṭhayāe dasame aṃge, ege suyakkhaṃdhe, paṇayālīsaṃ uddesaṇakālā, paṇayālīsaṃ samuddesaṇakālā, saṃkhenjāṇi payasayasahassāṇi payaggeṇaṃ paṇṇattāī| saṃkhejjā akkharā, aṇaṃtā gamā, aṇaṃtā pajjavā, parittā tasā, aṇaṃtā thāvarā, sāsayā kaḍā ṇibaddhā ṇikāiyā jiṇapaṇṇattā bhāvā āghavijaṃti paṇṇavinjaṃsi parūvijaṃti nidaṃsijaṃti uvadaṃsijjaṃti| se evaṃ āyā, se evaṃ ṇāyā, evaṃ viṇṇāyā, evaṃ caraṇa-karaṇaparūvaṇayā āghavijaṃti0| se taṃ paṇhāvāgaraṇāī 10||
praśnavyākaraṇa aṃgarūpa se daśavāṃ aṃga hai, isameṃ eka śrutaskandha hai, paiṃtālīsa uddeśana-kāla haiṃ, paiṃtālīsa samuddeśana-kāla haiṃ | pada-gaṇanā kī apekṣā saṃkhyāta lākha pada kahe gaye haiṃ| isameṃ saṃkhyāta akṣara haiṃ, ananta gama haiṃ, ananta paryāya haiṃ, parīta trasa haiṃ, ananta sthāvara haiṃ, isameṃ śāśvata, kṛta, nibaddha, nikācita jina-prajñapta bhāva kahe jāte haiṃ, prajñāpita kiye jāte haiṃ, prarūpita kiye jāte haiṃ, nidarśita kiye jāte haiṃ, aura upadirśata kiye jāte haiṃ| isa aṃga ke dvārā ātmā jñātā hotā hai, vijñātā hotā hai| isa prakāra caraṇa aura karaṇa kī prarūpaṇā ke dvārā vastu-svarūpa kā kathana, prajñāpana, nidarśana aura upadarśana kiyā jātā hai| yaha daśaveṃ praśnavyākaraṇa aṃga kā paricaya hai 10|
550-se kiṃ taṃ vivāgasuyaṃ? vivāgasue ṇaṃ sukkaḍa-dukkaḍāṇaṃ kammāṇaṃ phalavivāge āghavijjati| se samāsao duvihe paṇṇatte| taṃ jahā-duhavivāge ceva, suhavivāge ceva, tattha ṇaṃ dasa duhavivāgāṇi, dasa suhavivāgāṇi|
viṃpākasūtra kyā hai - isameṃ kyā varṇana hai?
vipākasūtra meṃ sukṛta (puṇya) aura duṣkṛta (pāpa) karmoṃ kā phala-vipāka kahā gayā hai| yaha vipāka saṃkṣepa se do prakāra kā hai-duḥkha-vipāka aura sukha-vipāka| inameṃ duḥkha-vipāka meṃ daśa adhyayana haiṃ aura sukha-vipāka meṃ bhī daśa adhyayana haiṃ|
551-se kiṃ taṃ duhavivāgāṇi? duhavivāgesu ṇaṃ duhavivāgāṇaṃ nagarāī ujjāṇāī ceiyāī vaṇakhaṃḍā rāyāṇo ammā-piyaro samosaraṇāī dhammāyariyā dhammakahāo nagaragamaṇāī saṃsārapabaṃdhe duhaparaṃparāo ya āghavijaṃti| se taṃ duhavivāgāṇi|
yaha duḥkhavipāka kyā hai - isameṃ kyā varṇana hai?
duḥkhavipāka meṃ duṣkṛtoṃ ke duḥkharūpa phaloṃ ko bhoganevāloṃ ke nagara, udyāna, caitya, vanakhaṇḍa, rājā, mātā-pitā, samavasaraṇa, dharmācārya, dharmakathāeṃ (gautama svāmī kā bhikṣā ke lie) nagaragamana, (pāpa ke phala se) saṃsāra-prabandha meṃ paḍa़ kara duḥkha paramparāoṃ ko bhogane kā varṇana kiyā jātā hai| yaha duḥkhavipāka hai|

﻿________________

186]
[samavāyāṅgasūtra 552-se kiṃ taṃ suhavivāgāṇi ? suhavivāgesu suhavivāgāṇaṃ ṇagarāiṃ ujjāṇāī ceiyāī vaṇakhaṃḍā rāyāṇo ammā-piyaro samosaraṇāī dhammāyariyā dhammakahāo ihaloiya-paraloiyaiḍḍivisesā bhogapariccāyā pavvajjāo suyapariggahā tavovahāṇāiṃ pariyāgā paḍimāo saṃlehaṇāo bhattapaccakkhāṇāiṃ pāovagamaṇāī devalogagamaṇāī sukulapaccāyāī puṇabohilāhā aṃtakiriyāo ya āpavijaṃti|
sukhavipāka kyā hai - isameṃ kyā varṇana hai ?
sukhavipāka meṃ sukṛtoṃ ke sukharūpa phaloṃ ko bhoganevāloṃ ke nagara, udyāna, caitya, vanakhaṇḍa, rājā, mātā-pitā, samavasaraṇa, dharmācārya, dharmakathāeṃ, ihalaukika-pāralaukika ṛddhiviśeṣa, bhoga-parityāga, pravrajyā, śruta-parigraha, tapa-upadhāna, dīkṣā paryāya, pratimāeṃ, saṃlekhanāeṃ, bhaktapratyākhyāna, pādapopagamana, devaloka-gamana, sukula-pratyāgamana, puna: bodhilābha aura unakī antakriyāeṃ kahī gaī haiṃ|
553–duhavivāgesuṇaṃ pāṇāivāya-aliyavayaṇa-corikkaraṇa-paradāramehuṇa-sasaṃgayāe mahativvakasāya-iṃdiyappamāya-pāvappaoya-asuhajjhavasāṇasaṃciyāṇaṃ kammāṇaṃ pāvagāṇaṃ pāva aṇubhāgaphalavivāgā ṇirayagati-tirikkhajoṇi-bahuvihavasaṇa-saya-paraṃparābaddhāṇaṃ maṇupatte vi āgayāṇaṃ jahā pāvakammaseseṇa pāvagā hoti phalavivāgā vaha-vasaṇa-viṇāsa-nāsā-kannuṭuṃguṭṭhakara-caraṇa-nahaccheyaṇa jibbha-ccheaṇa-aṃjaṇakaḍaggidāha-gayacalaṇa-malaṇa-phalāṇa-ullaṃvaṇasūlalayā-lauḍa-laṭṭhi-bhaṃjaṇa-tausīsa-gatattella-kalakala-ahisiṃcaṇa-kuṃbhipāga-kaṃpaṇathirabaṃdhaṇa-veha-vajjha-kattaṇa-patibhaya-kara-karapalīvaṇādi-dāruṇāṇi dukkhāṇi aṇovamāṇi bahuvivihaparaṃparāṇubaddhā ṇa muccaṃti pāvakammavallīe| aveyaittā hu ṇasthi mokkho taveṇa dhiidhaṇiyabaddhakaccheṇa sohaṇaṃ tassa vāvi hujjā|
duḥkhavipāka ke prāṇātipāta, asatya vacana, steya, para-dāra-maithuna, sasaṃgatā (parigraha-saṃcaya) mahātīvra kaṣāya, indriya-viṣaya-sevana, pramāda, pāpa-prayoga aura aśubha adhyavasānoṃ (pariṇāmoṃ) se saṃcita pāpakarmoṃ ke una pāparūpa anubhāga-phala-vipākoṃ kā varṇana kiyā gayā hai jinheṃ narakagati, aura tiryaga-yoni meṃ bahata prakāra ke saikaḍa़oṃ saṃkaṭoṃ kī paramparā meṃ paḍakara bhoganā paḍatā hai| vahā~ se nikala kara manuṣya bhava meṃ āne para bhī jīvoṃ ko pāpa-karmoṃ ke śeṣa rahane se aneka pāparūpa aśubha phalavipāka bhogane paḍa़te haiṃ, jaise-vadha (daṇḍa ādi se tāḍa़na) vṛṣaṇa-vināśa (napuṃsakīkaraṇa), nāsā-kartana, karṇakartana, oṣṭha-chedana, aṃguṣṭha-chedana, hasta-kartana, caraṇa-chedana, nakha-chedana, jihvā-chedana-aṃjana-dāha (uṣṇa lohaśalākā se āṃkhoṃ ko āṃjanā-phoḍa़nā), kaṭāgnidāha (vāṃsa se banī caṭāī se śarīra ko sarva ora se lapeṭa kara jalānā), hāthī ke pairoṃ ke nīce ḍālakara śarīra ko kucalavānā, pharase ādi se śarīra ko phāḍa़nā, rassiyoṃ se bā~dhakara vṛkṣoṃ para laṭakānā, triśūla-latā, lakuṭa (mūṃṭha vālā ḍaṃḍā) aura lakaḍa़ī se śarīra ko bhagna karanā, tape hue kaḍa़kaḍa़āte rāṃgā, sīsā evaṃ tela se śarīra kā abhisiṃcana karanā, kumbhī (loha-bhaṭṭī) meṃ pakānā, śītakāla meṃ śarīra para kapakaṃpī paidā karane vālā atiśītala jala ḍālanā, kāṣṭha ādi meṃ paira phaṃsākara sthita (dṛḍha़) bā~dhanā, bhāle ādi śastroṃ se chedana-bhedana karanā, varddhakartana (śarīra kī khāla udheḍa़nā) ati bhaya-kāraka kara-pradīpana (vastra lepaṭakara aura śarīra para tela ḍālakara donoṃ hāthoṃ meṃ

[samavāyāṅgasūtra 562-se kiṃ taṃ suttāī? suttāiṃ aṭṭhāsīti bhavaṃtītimakkhāyāiṃ| taṃ jahā-ujugaṃ pariṇayāpariṇayaṃ bahubhaṃgiyaṃ vippaccaiyaṃ [vina (ja) yacariyaṃ ] aṇaṃtaraṃ paraṃparaṃ samāṇaṃ saṃjūhaṃ (māsāṇaṃ) saṃbhinnaṃ āhaccāyaṃ (ahavvāyaṃ) sovatthi (vatta)yaṃ ṇaṃdāvattaṃ bahulaṃ puṭṭhāpuḍhe viyāvattaṃ evaṃbhūyaṃ duāvattaṃ vattamāṇappayaṃ samabhirūḍhaṃ savvao bhaī paṇāsaṃ (paṇṇāsaṃ) dupaḍiggahaṃ icceyāī vāvīsaṃ suttāī chiṇṇacheaṇaiāī sasamaya-suttaparivāḍīe, icceāiṃvāvīsaṃ suttāiṃ achinnacheyanaiyāī ājīviyasuttaparivāḍīe, icceāī vāvīsaṃ suttāī tikaṇaiyāī terāsiyasuttaparivāḍīe, icceāī vāvīsaṃ suttāiṃ caukkaṇaiyāī sasamayasuttaparivāḍīe| evāmeva sapuvvāvareṇa aṭṭhāsīti suttāī bhavaṃtītimakkhayāī| se ttaṃ suttāī|
sūtra kā svarūpa kyā hai? sūtra aṭhāsī hote haiṃ, aisā kahā gayā hai, jaise-1. ṛjuka, 2 pariṇatāpariṇata 3 bahubhaṃgika, 4 vijayacarcā, 5 anantara, 6 parampara, 7 samāna (samānasa),8 saṃjūha-saṃyūtha (jūha),9 saṃbhinna, 10 ahāccaya, 11 sauvastikaṃ, 12 nandyāvarta, 13 bahula, 14 pṛṣṭāpṛṣṭa, 15 vyāvṛtta, 16 evaṃbhūta, 17 dvayāvartta, 18 vartamānātmaka, 19 samabhirūḍha, 20 sarvatobhadra, 21 paṇāma (paṇṇāsa) aura 22 duṣpratigraha| ye bāīsa sūtra svasamayasūtra paripāṭī se chinnacchedanayika haiṃ| ye hī bāīsa sūtra ājīvikasūtraparipāṭī se acchinnachedanayika haiṃ| ye hī bāīsa sūtra trairāśikasūtraparipāṭī se trikanayika haiṃ aura ye hī bāīsa sūtra svasamaya sūtraparipāṭī se catuṣkanayika haiṃ| isa prakāra ye saba pūrvāpara bheda milakara aṭhāsī sūtra hote haiṃ, aisā kahā gayā hai| yaha sūtra nāma kā dūsarā bheda hai|
vivecana-jo naya sūtra ko chinna arthāt bheda se svīkāra kare, vaha chinnacchedanaya kahalātā hai| jaise-'dhammo maṃgalamukkiḻaṃ' ityādi śloka sūtra aura artha kī apekṣā apane artha ke pratipādana karane meṃ kisī dūsare śloka kī apekṣā nahīṃ rakhatā hai| kintu jo śloka apane artha ke pratipādana meṃ āge yā pīche ke śloka kī apekṣā rakhatā hai, vaha acchinnacchedanayika kahalātā hai| gośālaka ādi dravyārthika, paryāyārthika aura ubhayārthika ina tīna nayoṃ ko mānate haiṃ, ataḥ unheṃ trikanayika kahā gayā hai| kintu jo saṃgraha, vyavahāra, ṛjusūtra aura śabda naya ina cāra nayoṃ ko mānate haiṃ, unheṃ catuṣkanayika kahate haiṃ| trikanayika vāle sabhī padārthoṃ kā nirūpaṇa sat, asat aura ubhayātmaka rūpa se karate haiṃ| kintu catuṣkanayika vāle ukta cāra nayoṃ se sarva padārthoṃ kā nirūpaṇa karate haiṃ|
563-se kiṃ taṃ puvvagayaṃ ? puvvagayaṃ cauddasavihaṃ pannattaṃ, taṃ jahā-uppāyapuvvaṃ aggeṇīyaṃ vīriyaṃ atthinatthippavāyaṃ nāṇappavāyaṃ saccappavāyaṃ āyappavāyaṃ kammappavāyaṃ paccakkhāṇappavāyaṃ vijjāṇuppavāyaṃ abajhaṃ pāṇāū kiriyāvisālaṃ logabindusāraṃ 14|
yaha pūrvagata kyā hai-isameṃ kyā varṇana hai? __ pūrvagata caudaha prakāra ke kahe gaye haiṃ| jaise-1. utpādapūrva, 2. agrāyaṇīpūrva, 3. vīryapravādapūrva, 4. astināstipravādapūrva, 5. jñānapravādapūrva, 6. satyapravādapūrva, 7. ātmapravādapūrva, 8. karmapravādapūrva, 9. pratyākhyānapravādapūrva 10 vidyānupravādapūrva, 11. abandhyapūrva, 12. prāṇāyupūrva, 13. kriyāviśāla pūrva aura 14 lokabindusārapūrva|

[191 564-uppāyapuvvassa ṇaṃ dasa vatthū paṇṇattā| cattāri cūliyāvatthū paṇṇattā| aggeṇiyassa ṇaṃ puvvassa coddasa vatthū, vārasa cūliyāvatthū paṇṇattā| vīriyappavāyassa ṇaṃ puvvassa aṭṭha vatthū aṭṭa culiyāvatthū paṇṇattā| asthiṇatthippavāyassa ṇaṃ puvvassa aṭṭhārasa vattha dasa caliyāvattha paṇṇattā| nāṇappavāyassa ṇaṃ puvvassa bārasa vatthū paṇṇattā| saccappavāyassa ṇaṃ puvvassa do vatthū paṇṇattā| āyappavāyassa ṇaṃ puvvassa solasa vatthū paṇṇattā| kammappavāyapuvvassa ṇaṃ tīsaṃ vatthū paṇṇattā| paccakkhāṇassa ṇaṃ puvvassa vīsaṃ vatthū paṇṇattā| vijjāṇuṣpavāyassa ṇaṃ puvvassa pannarasa vatthū paṇṇattā| abaṃjhassa ṇaṃ puvvassa bārasa vatthū paṇṇattā| pāṇāussa ṇaṃ puvvassa terasa vatthū paṇṇattā| kiriyāvisālassa ṇaṃ puvvassa tīsaṃ vatthū paṇṇattā| logabindusārassa ṇaṃ puvvassa paṇavīsaṃ vatthū paṇṇattā|

566-se kiṃ taṃ aṇuoge? aṇuoge duvihe paṇṇatte| taṃ jahā-mūlapaḍhamāṇuoge ya gaṃḍiyāṇuoge ya| se kiṃ taṃ mūlapaḍhamāṇuoge? ettha ṇaṃ arahaṃtāṇaṃ bhagavaṃtāṇaṃ puvvabhavā devalogagamaṇāṇi āuṃcavaṇāṇi jammaṇāṇi a abhiseyā rāyavarasirīo sīyāo pavvajjāvo tavā ya bhattā kevalaṇāṇuppāyā a titthapavattaṇāṇi asaṃghayaṇaṃ saṃṭhāṇaṃ uccattaṃ āuṃ vanavibhāgo sīsā gaṇā gaṇaharā ya ajjā pavattaṇīo saṃghassa cauvvihassa jaṃ vāvi pariṇāmaṃ jiṇa-maṇapajjavaohināṇa-sammatta-suyanāṇiṇo ya vāī aṇuttaragaī ya jattiyā siddhā pāovagaā ya je jahiṃ jattiyāī cheaittā aṃtagaḍā muṇivaruttamā tama-raoghavippamukkā siddhipahamaṇuttaraṃ ca pattā, ee anne ya evamāiyā bhāvā mūlapaḍhamāṇuoge kahiā āghavijaṃti paṇṇavijjati parūvijjaṃti nidaṃsirjati uvadaṃsijati| se ttaṃ mūlapaḍhamāṇuoge|

[ samavāyāṅgasūtra hai, isa prakāra caraṇa aura karaṇa kī prarūpaṇā ke dvārā vastu-svarūpa kā kathana, prajñāpana, nidarśana aura upadarśana kiyā jātā hai | yaha bārahavā~ dṛṣṭivāda aṃga hai | yaha dvādaśāṅga gaṇi-piṭaka kā varṇana hai 12 |
571 – icceiyaṃ duvālasaṃgaṃ gaṇipiḍagaṃ atītakāle aṇaṃtā jīvā āṇāe virāhittā cāuraṃtasaṃsārakaṃtāraṃ aṇupariyaṭṭisu | icceiyaṃ duvālasaṃgaṃ gaṇipiḍagaṃ paḍuppaṇe kāle parittā jīvā āṇāe virāhittā cāuraṃtasaṃsārakaṃtāraṃ aṇupariyaṭṭhati | icceiyaṃ duvālasaṃgaṃ gaṇipiḍagaṃ aṇāgae kāle atā jīvā āṇāe virāhittā cāuraṃtasaṃsārakaṃtāraṃ aṇupariyaṭṭassaṃti |
isa dvādaśāṅga gaṇipiṭaka kī sūtrarūpa, artharūpa aura ubhaya rūpa ājñā kā virādhana karake arthāt durāgraha ke vaśībhūta hokara anyathā sūtrapāṭha karake, anyathā artha kathana karake aura anyathā sūtrārtha - ubhaya kī prarūpaṇā karake ananta jīvoṃ ne bhūtakāla meṃ caturgatirūpa saṃsāra - kāntāra (gahana vana) meṃ paribhramaṇa kiyā hai, isa dvādaśāṅga gaṇi-piṭaka kī sūtra, artha aura ubhaya rūpa ājñā kā virādhana karake vartamāna kāla meṃ parīta (parimita) jīva caturgatirūpa saṃsāra- kāntāra meṃ paribhramaṇa kara rahe haiṃ aura isī dvādaśāṅga gaṇipiṭaka kī sūtra, artha aura ubhayarūpa ājñā kā virādhana kara bhaviṣyakāla meṃ ananta jīva caturgatirūpa saṃsāra - kāntāra meṃ paribhramaṇa kareṃge|
572 – icceiyaṃ duvālasaṃgaṃ gaṇipiḍagaṃ atītakāle aṇaṃtā jīvā āṇāe ārāhit cāuraṃtasaṃsārakaṃtāraṃ vīīvaiṃsu | evaṃ paḍuppaṇṇe'vi [ parittā jīvā āṇāe ārāhittā cāuraṃtasaṃsārakaṃtāraṃ vīīvaṃti ] evaṃ aṇāgae vi [ aṇaṃtā jīvā āṇāe ārāhittā cāuraṃtasaṃsārakaṃtāraṃ vīīvaissaṃti ] |
isa dvādaśāṅga gaṇi-piṭaka kī sūtra, artha aura ubhayarūpa ājñā kā ārādhana karake ananta jīvoṃ ne bhūtakāla meṃ caturgati rūpa saṃsāra - kāntāra ko pāra kiyā hai ( mukti ko prāpta kiyā hai) | vartamāna kāla meṃ bhī (parimita) jīva isa dvādaśāṅga gaṇi-piṭaka kī sūtra, artha aura ubhaya rūpa ājñā kā ārādhana karake caturgatirūpa saṃsāra-kāntāra ko pāra kara rahe haiṃ aura bhaviṣyakāla meṃ bhī ananta jīva isa dvādaśāṅga gaṇipiṭaka kī sūtra, artha aura ubhaya rūpa ājñā kā ārādhana karake caturgatirūpa saṃsāra - kāntāra ko pāra kareṃge |
573 – duvālasaṃge ṇaṃ gaṇipiḍage ṇa kayāi ṇāsī, ṇa kayāvi ṇatthi, ṇa kayāi bhavissai| bhuṃvi ca, bhavati ya, bhavissati ya | dhuve nitie sāsae akkhae avvae avaṭṭhie ṇicce | se jahā ṇāmae paṃca atthikāyā ṇa kayāi ṇa āsi, ṇa kayāi ṇatthi, ṇa kayāi ṇa bhavissaṃti | bhuviṃca, bhavati ya, bhavissaṃti ya, dhuvā ṇitiyā sāsayā akkhayā avvayā avaṭṭhiyā ṇiccā | evāmeva duvālasaṃge gaṇipiḍage ṇa kayāi ṇa āsi, ṇa kayāi ṇatthi, ṇa kayāi ṇa bhavissai | bhuvaṃ ca bhavati ya, bhavissai ya | dhuve jāva avaṭṭhie ṇicce |

[199 vivecana-āge dī gaī gāthā saṃkhyā eka ke anusāra dūsarī pṛthivī eka lākha battīsa hajāra yojana moṭī hai| usake eka hajāra yojana ūpara kā aura eka hajāra yojana nīce kā bhāga choḍa़kara madhyavartī eka lākha tīsa hajāra yojana bhū-bhāga meṃ paccīsa lākha nārakāvāsa haiṃ| tīsarī pṛthivī eka lākha aṭṭhāīsa hajāra yojana moṭī hai| usake eka hajāra yojana ūpara aura eka hajāra yojana nīce kā bhāga choḍa़kara madhyavartī eka lākha chabbīsa hajāra yojana bhū-bhāga meṃ pandraha lākha nārakāvāsa haiṃ | cauthī pṛthivī eka lākha bīsa hajāra yojana moṭī hai| usake ūpara tathā nīce kī eka eka hajāra yojana bhūmi ko choḍa़kara śeṣa eka lākha aṭhāraha hajāra yojana bhū-bhāga meṃ daśa lākha nārakāvāsa haiṃ| pāṃcavīṃ pṛthivī eka lākha aṭhāraha hajāra yojana moṭī hai| usake eka eka hajāra yojana ūparī vā nīce kā bhāga choḍakara śeṣa madhyavartī eka lākha solaha hajāra yojana bhū-bhāga meṃ tīna lākha nārakāvāsa haiṃ| chaṭhī pṛthivī eka lākha solaha hajāra yojana moṭī hai, usake eka-eka hajāra yojana ūparī aura nīce kā bhāga choḍa़kara madhyavartī eka lākha caudaha hajāra yojana bhū-bhāga meṃ pāṃca kama eka lākha (99995) nārakāvāsa haiṃ | sātavīṃ pṛthivī eka lākha āṭha hajāra yojana moṭī hai| usake sāḍhe bāvana, sāḍhe bāvana hajāra yojana ūparī tathā nīce ke bhāga ko choḍa़kara madhya meṃ pāṃca nārakāvāsa haiṃ| usameṃ apratiṣṭhāna nāma kā nārakavāsa ṭhīka cāroṃ nārakāvāsoṃ ke madhya meṃ he aura śeṣa kāla, mahākāla, rauruka aura mahārauruka nārakavāsa usakī cāroṃ diśāoṃ meṃ avasthita haiṃ|
sabhī pṛthiviyoṃ meṃ nārakāvāsa tīna prakāra ke haiṃ – indraka, śreṇībaddha (āvalikāpraviṣṭa) aura puṣpaprakīrṇaka (āvalikābāhya)| indraka nārakavāsa sabake bīca meṃ hotā hai aura śreṇībaddha nārakavāsa usakī āṭhoṃ diśāoṃ meṃ avasthita haiṃ| puṣpaprakīrṇaka yā āvalikābāhya nārakāvāsa śreṇibaddha nārakāvāsoṃ ke madhya meṃ avasthita haiṃ| indraka nārakāvāsa gola hote haiṃ aura śeṣa nārakāvāsa trikoṇa catuṣkoṇa ādi nānā ākāra vāle kahe gaye haiṃ tathā nīce kī ora sabhī nārakavāsa kṣurapra (khurapā) ke ākāra vāle haiṃ| 583
āsīyaṃ battīsaṃ aṭṭhāvīsaṃ taheva vīsaṃ ca | aṭṭhārasa solasagaṃ aṭhuttarameva bāhallaṃ ||1|| tīsā ya paṇṇavīsā pannarasa daseva sayasahassāī | tiṇṇegaṃ paṃcūṇaṃ paṃceva aṇuttarā naragā ||2|| causaṭṭhī asurāṇaṃ caurāsīiṃ ca hoi nāgāṇaṃ | vāvattari suvannāṇaṃ vāukumārāṇa chaṇṇauī ||3|| dīva-disā-udahīṇaṃ vijjukumārida-thaṇiyamaggīṇaṃ || chaṇhaṃ pi juvalayāṇaṃ chāvattarimo ya sayasahassā ||4|| battīsāvīsā vārasa aḍa cauro ya sayasahassā | paṇṇā cattālīsā chacca sayā sahassāre ||5|| āṇaya-pāṇayakappe cattāri sayā''raṇaccue tini | satta vimāṇasayāī causu vi eesu kappesu ||6|| ekkārasuttaraṃ heṭṭhimesu sattuttaraṃ ca majjhimae | sayamegaṃ uvarimae paṃceva aṇuttara vimāṇā ||7||

[samavāyāṅgasūtra ratnaprabhā pṛthivī kā bāhalya (moṭāī) eka lākha assī hajāra yojana hai| śarkarā pṛthivī kā bāhalya eka lākha battīsa hajāra yojana hai| bālukā pṛthivī kā bāhalya eka lākha aṭṭhāīsa hajāra yojana hai| paṃkaprabhā pṛthivī kā bāhalya eka lākha vīsa hajāra yojana hai| dhūmaprabhā pṛthivī kā bāhalya eka lākha aṭṭhāraha hajāra yojana hai| tamaḥprabhā pṛthivī kā bāhalya eka lākha solaha hajāra yojana hai aura mahātamaḥprabhā pṛthivī kā bāhalya eka lākha āṭha hajāra yojana hai ||1||
ratnaprabhā pṛthivī meṃ tīsa lākha nārakāvāsa haiṃ| śarkarā pṛthivī meṃ paccīsa lākha nārakavāsa haiṃ| vālukā pṛthivī meṃ pandraha lākha nārakāvāsa haiṃ| paṃkaprabhā pṛthivī meṃ daśa lākha nārakāvāsa haiṃ| dhūmaprabhā pṛthivī meṃ tīna lākha nārakāvāsa haiṃ| tamaḥprabhā pṛthivī meṃ pāṃca kama eka lākha nārakāvāsa haiṃ| mahātamaḥ pṛthivī meṃ (kevala) pāṃca anuttara nārakāvāsa haiṃ ||2||
asurakumāroṃ ke causaṭha lākha bhavana haiṃ| nāgakumāroṃ ke caurāsī lākha bhavana haiṃ| suparṇakumāroṃ ke bahattara lākha bhavana haiṃ| vāyukumāroṃ ke jhyānavai lākha bhavana haiṃ ||3||

saudharmakalpa meṃ battīsa lākha vimāna haiṃ| īśānakalpa meṃ aṭṭhāīsa lākha vimāna haiṃ | sanatkumārakalpa meṃ bāraha lākha vimāna haiṃ| māhendrakalpa meṃ āṭha lākha vimāna haiṃ| brahmakalpa meṃ cāra lākha vimāna haiṃ| lāntakakalpa meṃ pacāsa hajāra vimāna haiṃ| mahāśukra vimāna meṃ cālīsa hajāra vimāna haiṃ| sahasrārakalpa meṃ chaha hajāra vimāna haiṃ ||5||

[201 paṇṇattā, taṃ jahā-kāle mahākāle rorue mahārorue apaiṭṭhāṇe nāmaṃ paṃcame| te ṇaṃ nirayā vaṭe ya taṃsā ya| ahe khurappasaṃṭhāṇasaṃṭhiyā jāva asubhā naragā, asubhāo naraesu veyaṇāo|
sātavīṃ pṛthivī meṃ pṛcchā- [bhagavan ! sātavīṃ pṛthivī meṃ kitanā kṣetra avagāhana kara kitane nārakāvāsa haiṃ?]
gautama! eka lākha āṭha hajāra yojana bāhalyavālī sātavīṃ pṛthivī meṃ ūpara se sāḍha़e bāvana hajāra yojana avagāhana kara aura nīce bhī sāḍha़e bāvana hajāra yojana choḍa़kara madhyavartī tīna hajāra yojana meṃ sātavīṃ pathivī ke nārakiyoṃ ke pāṃca anuttara, bahuta viśāla mahānaraka kahe gaye haiṃ, jaise-kāla, mahākāla, roruka mahāroruka aura pāṃcavāṃ apratiṣṭhāna nāma kā naraka hai| ye naraka vatta (gola) aura trayasta haiṃ. arthāta madhyavartī apratiṣṭhāna naraka gola ākāra vālā hai aura śeṣa cāroṃ diśāvartī cāroṃ naraka trikoṇa ākāra vāle haiṃ| nīce tala bhāga meṃ ve naraka kṣurapra (khurapā) ke ākāra vāle haiṃ|.....yāvat ye naraka aśubha haiṃ aura ina narakoṃ meṃ aśubha vedanāe haiṃ|
586-kevaiyā ṇaṃ bhaṃte! asurakumārāvāsā paṇṇattā? goyamā! imīse ṇaṃ rayaṇappabhāe puḍhavīe asīuttara joyaṇasayasahassa-bāhallāe uvari egaṃ joyaṇasahassaṃ ogāhettā heṭṭhā cegaṃ joyaṇa-sahassaṃ vajjittā majjhe aṭṭhahattari joyaṇasayasahasse ettha ṇaṃ rayaṇappabhāe puḍhavīe causaddhiṃ asurakumārāvāsasayasahassā paṇṇattā| te ṇaṃ bhavaṇā bāhiṃ vaṭṭā, aṃto cauraṃsā, ahe pokkharakaṇṇiā-saṃṭhāṇasaṃṭhiyā ukkiṇṇaṃtara viula-gaṃbhīra-khāya-phalihā aṭṭālaya-cariṣadāra-goura-kavāḍa-toraṇa-paḍiduvāra-desabhāgā jaṃta-musala-bhusaṃḍha-sayagghi-parivāriyā agjhā aḍayālakoṭṭharaiyā aḍayālakayavaṇamālā lāulloiyamahiyā gosīsa-sarasa-rattacaṃdaṇa-daddaradiṇṇapaṃcaṃgulitalā kālāguru-pavarakuṃdurukka turukkaḍajhaṃta-dhūvamaghamaghetagaṃdhuddhayābhirāmā sugaṃdhiyā gaṃdhavaṭṭibhūyā acchā saṇhā laṇhā ghaṭṭhā maṭṭhā nīrayā ṇimmalā vitimirā visuddhā sappabhā samarīyā saujjoyā pāsāīyā darisaṇijjā abhirūvā paḍirūvā| evaṃ jaṃ jassa kamatī taṃ tassa, jaṃ jaṃgāhāhiṃ bhaṇiyaṃ taha ceva vaṇṇao|
bhagavan ! asurakumāroṃ ke āvāsa (bhavana) kitane kahe gaye haiṃ?
gautama! isa eka lākha assī hajāra yojana bāhalyavālī ratnaprabhā pṛthivī meṃ ūpara se eka hajāra yojana avagāhana kara aura nīce eka hajāra yojana choḍa़kara madhyavartī eka lākha aṭhahattara hajāra yojana meṃ ratnaprabhā pṛthivī ke bhītara asurakumāroṃ ke causaṭha lākha bhavanāvāsa kahe gaye haiṃ| ve bhavana bāhara gola haiṃ, bhītara caukoṇa haiṃ aura nīce kamala kī karṇikā ke ākāra se sthita haiṃ | unake cāroṃ ora khāī aura parikhā khudī huī haiṃ jo bahuta gaharī haiṃ | khāī aura parikhā ke madhya meṃ pāla baṃdhī huī hai| tathā ve bhavana aṭṭālaka, carikā, dvāra, gopura, kapāṭa, toraṇa, pratidvāra, deśa rūpa bhāga vāle haiṃ, yaṃtra, mūsala, bhusuṃḍhī, śataghnī, ina śastroṃ se saṃyukta haiṃ | śatruoṃ kī senāoṃ se ajeya haiṃ | aḍa़tālīsa koṭhoṃ se racita,aḍa़tālīsa vana-mālāoṃ se śobhita haiṃ| unake bhūmibhāga aura bhittiyā~ uttama lepoṃ se lipī aura cikanī haiṃ, gośīrṣacandana aura

[ samavāyāṅgasūtra lālacandana ke sarasa sugandhita lepa se una bhavanoṃ kī bhittiyoṃ para pāṃcoṃ aṃguliyoṃ yukta hastatala (hātha) aṃkita haiṃ| isī prakāra bhavanoṃ kī sīḍha़iyoṃ para bhī gośīrṣacandana aura lālacandana ke rasa se pāṃcoṃ aṃguliyoṃ ke hastatala aṃkita haiṃ| ve bhavana kālāguru, pradhāna kundaru aura turuṣka (lobhāna) yukta dhūpa ke jalate rahane se maghamaghāyamāna, sugandhita aura sundaratā se abhirāma ( manohara) haiṃ| vahāṃ sugandhita agarabattiyāṃ jala rahī haiṃ| ve bhavana ākāśa ke samāna svaccha haiṃ, sphaṭika ke samāna kāntiyukta haiṃ, atyanta cikane haiṃ, ghise hue haiṃ, paॉliśa kiye hue haiṃ, nīraja (raja - dhūli se rahita haiṃ) nirmala haiṃ, andhakāra - rahita haiṃ, viśuddha (niṣkalaṃka) haiṃ, prabhā - yukta haiṃ, marīciyoṃ (kiraṇoṃ se ) yukta haiṃ, udyota (śītala prakāśa) se yukta haiṃ, mana ko prasanna karane vāle haiṃ | darśanīya (dekhane ke yogya) haiṃ, abhirūpa (kānta, sundara ) haiṃ aura pratirūpa ( ramaṇīya) haiṃ|
jisa prakāra se asurakumāroṃ ke bhavanoṃ kā varṇana kiyā gayā hai, usī prakāra nāgakumāra ādi śeṣa bhavanavāsī devoṃ ke bhavanoṃ kā bhī varṇana jahāṃ jaisā ghaṭita aura upayukta ho, vaisā karanā cāhie| tathā ūpara kahī gaī gāthāoṃ se jisake jitane bhavana batāye gaye haiṃ, unakā vaisā hī varṇana karanā cāhie|
587 - kevaiyā ṇaṃ bhaṃte! puḍhavikāiyāvāsā paṇṇattā? goyamā! asaṃkhejjā puḍhavikāiyā vāsā paṇṇattā| evaṃ jāva maṇussa tti |
bhagavan! pṛthivīkāyika jīvoṃ ke āvāsa kitane kahe gaye haiṃ?
gautama! pṛthivīkāyika jīvoṃ ke asaṃkhyāta āvāsa kahe gaye haiṃ | isī prakāra jalakāyika jīvoṃ se lekara yāvat manuṣyoṃ taka ke jānanā cāhie|
vivecana - garbhaja manuṣyoṃ ke āvāsa to saṃkhyāta hī hote haiṃ tathā sammūcchima manuṣyoṃ ke āvāsa nahīṃ hote haiṃ kintu pratyeka śarīra meṃ eka eka jīva hone se ve asaṃkhyāta haiṃ, itanā viśeṣa jānanā cāhie| 588 – kevaiyā ṇaṃ bhaṃte vāṇamaṃtarāvāsā paṇṇattā ? goyamā ! imīse ṇaṃ rayaṇappabhāe puḍhavīe rayaṇāmayassa kaṃḍassa-joyaṇasahassa - bāhallassa uvariṃ egaṃ joyaṇasayaṃ ogāhettā heṭṭha cegaṃ joyaṇasayaṃ vajjettā majjhe aṭṭhasu joyaṇasaesa ettha ṇaṃ vāṇamaṃtarāṇaṃ devāṇaṃ tiriyamasaṃkhejjā bhomejjā nagarāvāsasayasahassā paṇṇattā | te ṇaṃ bhomejjā nagarā bāhiṃ vaṭṭā aṃto cauraṃsā | evaṃ jahā bhavaṇavāsīṇaṃ taheva ṇeyavvā | ṇavaraṃ paḍāgamālāulā surammā pāsāīyā darisaṇijjā abhiruvā paḍirūvā |
bhagavan ! vānavyantaroṃ ke āvāsa kitane kahe gaye haiṃ?
gautama ! isa ratnaprabhā pṛthivī ke eka hajāra yojana moṭe ratnamaya kāṃḍa ke eka sau yojana ūpara se avagāhana kara aura eka sau yojana nīce ke bhāga ko choḍa़ kara madhya ke āṭha sau yojanoṃ meṃ vānavyantara devoṃ ke tirache phaile hue asaṃkhyāta lākha bhaumeyaka nagarāvāsa kahe gaye haiṃ | ve bhaumeyaka nagara bāhara gola aura bhītara caukora haiṃ | isa prakāra jaisā bhavanavāsī devoṃ ke bhavanoṃ kā varṇana kiyā gayā hai, vaisā hī varṇana vānavyantara devoṃ ke bhavanoṃ kā jānanā cāhie| kevala itanī viśeṣatā hai ki ye patākā- mālāoṃ se vyāpta haiṃ | yāvat suramya haiṃ, manaḥ ko prasanna karane vāle haiṃ, darśanīya haiṃ, abhirūpa haiṃ aura pratirūpa haiṃ|

[ 203
rayaṇappabhāe puḍhavīe bahusamaramaṇijjāo bhūmibhāgāo sattanauyāiṃ joyaṇasayāī uḍḍhaṃ uppaittā ettha ṇaṃ dasuttarajoyaṇasayabāhalle tiriyaṃ joisavisae joisiyāṇaṃ devāṇaṃ asaṃkhejjā joisiyavimāṇāvāsā paṇṇattā | te ṇaṃ joisiyavimāṇāvāsā abbhuggayabhūsiyapahasiyā vivihamaṇirayaṇabhatticittā vāuyavijaya - vejayaṃtī- paḍāga - chattāichattakaliyā tuṃgā gagaṇa talamaṇulihaṃtasiharā jālaṃtara - rayaṇapaṃjarummiliyavva maṇikaṇagathrubhiyāgā viyasiya sayapattapuṇḍarīya-tilaya - rayaṇaddhacaṃdacittā aṃto vāhiṃ ca saṇhā tavaṇijja-vāluā patthaḍā suhaphāsā sassirīyarūvā pasāiyā darisaṇijjā |
bhagavan ! jyotiṣka devoṃ ke vimānāvāsa kitane kahe gaye haiṃ ?
gautama! isa ratnaprabhā pṛthivī ke bahusama ramaṇīya bhūmibhāga se sāta sau nabbai yojana ūpara jākara eka sau daśa yojana bāhalya vāle tirache jyotiṣka-viṣayaka ākāśabhāga meṃ jyotiṣka devoṃ ke asaṃkhyāta vimānāvāsa kahe gaye haiṃ | ve apane meṃ se nikalatī huī aura sarva diśāoṃ meṃ phailatī huī prabhā se ujjvala haiṃ, aneka prakāra ke maṇi aura ratnoṃ kī citrakārī se yukta haiṃ, vāyu se uḍa़tī huī vijaya - vaijayantī patakāoṃ se aura chatrātichatroṃ se yukta haiṃ, gaganatala ko sparśa karane vāle ūṃce śikhara vāle haiṃ, unakī jāliyoṃ ke bhītara ratna lage hue haiṃ| jaise paṃjara (pracchādana) se tatkāla nikālī vastu saśrīka- camacamātī hai baise hī ve saśrīka | maṇi aura suvarṇa kī stūpikāoṃ se yukta haiṃ, vikasita śatapatroṃ evaṃ puṇḍarīkoṃ (śveta kamaloṃ) se, tilakoṃ se, ratnoṃ ke ardhacandrākāra citroṃ se vyāpta haiṃ, bhītara aura bāhara atyanta cikane haiṃ, tapāye hue suvarṇa ke samāna vālukāmayī prastaṭoṃ yā prastāroṃ vāle haiṃ | sukhada sparśa vāle haiṃ, śobhāyukta haiṃ, mana ko prasanna karane vāle aura darśanīya haiṃ|
590 - kevaiyā ṇaṃ bhaṃte! vemāṇiyāvāsā paṇṇattā ? goyamā ! imīse ṇaṃ rayaṇappabhāe puḍhavīe bahusamaramaṇijjāo bhūmibhāgāo uḍḍhaṃ caṃdima-sūriya-gahagaṇa - nakkhatta-tārārūvāṇaṃ vīivaittā bahūṇi joyaṇāṇi bahūṇi joyaṇasayāṇi bahūṇi joyaṇasahassāṇi [ bahūṇi joyaṇasaṃyasahassāṇi ] bahuio joyaṇakoḍīo bahuio joyaṇakoḍākoḍīa asaṃkhejjāo joyaṇakoḍākoḍīo uḍḍhaṃ dūraṃ vīivaittā ettha ṇaṃ vemāṇiyāṇaṃ devāṇaṃ sohammīsāṇaṃsaṇaṃkumāra- māhiṃda-baṃbha-laṃtaga-sukka sahassāra- āṇaya- pāṇaya- āraṇa-accuesu gevejjamaṇuttaresu ya caurāsīiṃ vimāṇāvāsasayasahassā sattāṇauī ca sahassā tevīsaṃ ca vimāṇā bhavaṃtītimakkhāyā|
bhagavan! vaimānika devoṃ ke kitane āvāsa kahe gaye haiṃ ?
gautama ! isī ratnaprabhā pṛthivī ke bahusama ramaṇīya bhūmibhāga se ūpara, candra, sūrya, grahagaṇa, nakṣatra aura tārakāoṃ ko ullaṃghana kara, aneka yojana, aneka śata yojana, aneka sahasra yojana [ aneka śatasahasra yojana] aneka koṭi yojana, aneka koṭākoṭī yojana aura asaṃkhyāta koṭā- koṭī yojana ūpara bahuta dūra taka ākāśa kā ullaṃghana kara saudharma, īśāna, sanatkumāra, māhendra, brahma, lāntaka, śukra, sahasrāra, ānata, prāṇata, āraṇa, acyuta kalpoṃ meṃ, gaiveyakoṃ meṃ aura anuttaroṃ meṃ vaimānika devoṃ ke caurāsī lakha sattānavai hajāra aura teīsa vimāna haiṃ, aisā kahā gayā hai|

592-sohamme ṇaṃ bhaṃte! kappe kevaiyā vimāṇāvāsā paṇṇattā ?

593-neraiyāṇaṃ bhaṃte! kevaiyaṃ kālaṃ ṭhiī panattā ? goyamā! jahanneṇaṃ dasavāsasahassāī, ukkoseṇaṃ tettīsaṃ sāgarovamāī ṭhiī pannattā| apajjattagāṇaṃ neraiyāṇaṃ bhaṃte! kevaiyaṃ kālaṃ ṭhiī patratā ? jahanneṇaṃ aṃtomuhurā, ukkoseṇaṃ vi aṃtomuhuttaṃ| pajjattagāṇaṃ jahanneṇaṃ dasavāsasahassāī aṃtomuhuttūṇāī, ukkoseṇaṃ tettīsaṃ sāgarovamāiṃ aṃtomuhuttūṇāī| imīse ṇaṃ rayaṇappabhāe puḍhavīe evaṃ jāva|
bhagavan ! nārakoṃ kī sthiti kitane kāla kī kahī gaī hai ? gautama! jaghanya sthiti daśa hajāra varṣa kī aura utkṛṣṭa sthiti tetīsa sāgaropama kī kahī gaī hai| bhagavana! aparyāptaka nārakoṃ kī kitane kāla kī sthiti kahī gaī hai? [gautama!] jaghanya bhī antarmuhūrta kī aura utkṛṣṭa bhī sthiti antarmuhūrta kī kahī gaī hai|
paryāptaka nārakiyoṃ kī jaghanya sthiti antarmuhūrta kama daśa hajāra varṣa kī aura utkṛṣṭa sthiti antarmuhūrta kama tetīsa sāgaropama kī hai| isī prakāra isa ratnaprabhā pṛthivī se lekara mahātamaḥprabhā pṛthivī taka aparyāptaka nārakiyoṃ kī jaghanya aura utkṛṣṭa sthiti antarmuhūrta kī tathā paryāptakoṃ kī sthiti vahā~ kī sāmānya, jaghanya aura utkṛṣṭi sthiti se antarmuhūrta antarmuhūrta kama jānanā cāhie|

[205 paryāptaka-aparyāptaka kāla-bhāvī jaghanya aura utkṛṣṭa sthiti prajñāpanāsūtra ke anusāra jānanā cāhie|]
594-vijaya-vejayaṃta-jayaṃta-aparājiyāṇaṃ devāṇaṃ kevaiyaṃ kālaṃ ṭhiī pannattā? goyamā! jahanneṇaṃ battīsaṃ sāgarovamāiṃ, ukkoseṇaṃ tettīsaṃ sāgarovamāiṃ| savvaḍhe ajahaṇṇamaṇukkoseṇaṃ tettīsaṃ sāgarovamāiṃ ṭhiī pannattā|
bhagavan ! vijaya, vaijayanta, jayanta, aparājita vimānavāsī devoṃ kī sthiti kitane kāla kī kahī gaī hai?
gautama! jaghanya sthiti battīsa sāgaropama aura utkṛṣṭa sthiti tetīsa sāgaropama kahī gaī hai|
sarvārthasiddha nāmaka anuttara vimānoṃ meṃ ajaghanya-anutkṛṣṭa (utkṛṣṭa aura jaghanya ke bheda se rahita) saba devoṃ kī tetīsa sāgaropama kī sthiti kahī gaī haiṃ|

595 –kati ṇaṃ bhaṃte! sarīrā pannattā? goyamā! paṃca sarīrā pannattā, taṃ jahā-orālie veuvvie āhārae teyae kammae|
bhagavan ! śarīra kitane kahe gaye haiṃ?
gautama! śarīra pāṃca kahe gaye haiṃ - audārika śarīra, vaikriya śarīra, āhāraka śarīra, taijasa śarīra aura kārmaṇa śarīra|
596 -orāliyasarīre ṇaṃ bhaṃte! kaivihe pannatte ? goyamā! paṃcavihe pannatte| taṃ jahāegiṃdiya-orāliyasarīre jāva gabbhavakkaṃtiya maṇussa-paṃcidiya-orāliyasarīre ya|
bhagavan ! audārika śarīra kitane prakāra ke kahe gaye haiṃ?
gautama! pāṃca prakāra ke kahe gaye haiṃ| jaise-ekendriya audārikaśarīra, yāvat [dvīndriya audārikaśarīra, trīndriya audārikaśarīra, caturindriya audārikaśarīra aura paṃcendriya audārikaśarīra | ityādi prajñāpanokta] garbhaja manuṣya paṃcendriya audārikaśarīra taka jānanā cāhie|
597-orāliyasarīrassaṇaṃ bhaṃte ! kemahāliyā sarīrogāhaṇā paṇṇattā? goyamā ! jahanneṇaṃ aṃgulaasaṃkhejjatibhāgaṃ, ukkoseṇaṃ sāiregaṃ joyaṇasahassaṃ evaṃ jahā ogāhaṇa-saṃṭhāṇe orāliyapamāṇaṃ taha niravasesaṃ (bhāṇiyavvaṃ|)|evaṃ jāva maṇusse tti ukkoseṇaṃ tiṇṇi gāuyāī|
bhagavan ! audārikaśarīra vāle jīva kī utkṛṣṭa śarīra-avagāhanā kitanī kahī gaī hai?
gautama! [pṛthivīkāyika ādi kī apekṣā] jaghanya śarīra-avagāhanā aṃgula ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga pramāṇa aura utkṛṣṭa śarīra-avagāhanā [bādara vanaspatikāyika kī apekṣā] kucha adhika eka hajāra yojana kahī gaī hai|

[ samavāyāṅgasūtra
isa prakāra jaise avagāhanā saṃsthāna nāmaka prajñāpanā- pada meṃ audārikaśarīra kī avagāhanā kā pramāṇa kahā gayā hai, vaisā hī yahāṃ sampūrṇa rūpa se kahanā cāhie| isa prakāra yāvat manuṣya kī utkṛṣṭa śarīra- avagāhanā tīna gavyūti (kośa) kahī gaī hai|
598 – kaivihe ṇaṃ bhaṃte! veḍavviyasarīre pannatte ? goyamā ! duvihe pannatte – egiṃdiyaveuvviyasarīre ya paṃciṃdiya - veḍavviyasarīre a | evaṃ jāva saṇakumāre āḍhattaṃ jāva anuttarāṇaṃ bhavadhāraṇijjā jāva tesiṃ rayaṇī parihāyai |
bhagavan! vaikriyikaśarīra kitane prakāra kā kahā gayā hai ?
gautama ! vaikriyikaśarīra do prakāra kā kahā gayā hai – ekendriya vaikriyikaśarīra aura paṃcendriya vaikiyikaśarīra |
isa prakāra yāvat sanatkumāra-kalpa se lekara anuttara vimānoṃ taka ke devoṃ kā vaikriyika bhavadhāraṇīya śarīra kahanā | vaha kramaśaḥ eka-eka rani kama hotā hai |
vivecana - vaikriyikaśarīra ekendriyoṃ meṃ kevala vāyukāyika jīvoṃ ke hī hotā hai| vikalendriya aura sammūcchima tiryaṃcoṃ ke vaha nahīṃ hotā | nārakoṃ meṃ, bhavanavāsī, vānavyantara, jyotiṣka devoṃ meṃ, saudharma īśāna kalpoṃ ke devoṃ meṃ aura sanatkumārakalpa se lekara anuttara vimānavāsī devoṃ taka vaikriyika śarīra hotā hai | nārakoṃ kā bhavadhāraṇīya śarīra sātaveṃ naraka meṃ pā~ca sau dhanuṣa se lekara ghaṭatā huā prathama naraka meṃ sāta dhanuṣa, tīna hātha aura chaha aṃgula hotā hai | bhavanavāsī, vānavyantara, jyotiṣka, saudharma aura īśāna kalpavāsī devoṃ kā bhavadhāraṇīya śarīra sāta ratni yā hātha hotā hai | sanatkumāra- māhendra devoṃ kā bhavadhāraṇīya śarīra chaha hātha hotā hai | brahma-lāntaka devoṃ kā pā~ca hātha, mahāśukra - sahasrāra devoṃ kā cāra hātha, ānata-prāṇata, āraṇa- acyuta devoṃ kā tīna hātha, graiveyaka devoṃ kā do hātha aura anuttara vimānavāsī devoṃ kā bhavadhāraṇīya śarīra eka hātha hotā hai| jo tiryaṃca garbhaja haiṃ, aura jo manuṣya garbhaja haiṃ, unake bhavadhāraṇīya vaikriyika śarīra nahīṃ hotā hai, kintu labdhipratyaya-janita vaikriyika śarīra hī kisī-kisī ke hotā hai| sabameṃ nahīṃ| unameṃ bhī vaha karmabhūmija, saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka aura paryāptaka jīvoṃ ke hī hotā hai | uttara - vaikriyika śarīra manuṣya ke utkṛṣṭa kucha adhika eka lākha yojana kī avagāhanāvālā hotā hai aura devoṃ ke eka lākha yojana avagāhanā vālā | tiryaṃcoṃ ke utkṛṣṭa sau pṛthaktva yojana avagāhanā vālā ho sakatā hai|
599–āhārayasarīre ṇaṃ bhaṃte! kaivihe pannatte ? goyamā ! egākāre pannatte |

[ samavāyāṅgasūtra
gautama! paryāpta saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya-āhārakaśarīra hai, aparyāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya - āhārakaśarīra nahīṃ hai|
208]
bhagavan! yadi vaha paryātaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika munaṣya - āhāraka śarīra hai, to kyā vaha samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyāta-varṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya āhārakaśarīra hai, athavā mithyādṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya āhāraka śarīra hai athavā samyagmithyādṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyāta varṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya- āhāraka śarīra hai ?
gautama ! vaha samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya- āhāraka śarīra hai, na mithyādṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya āhāraka śarīra hai aura na samyagmithyādṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya- āhāraka śarīra hai|
bhagavan ! yadi vaha samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣyaāhārakaśarīra hai, to kyā vaha saṃyata samyagdṛṣṭiparyāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣyaāhārakaśarīra hai, athavā asaṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣyaāhārakaśarīra hai athavā saṃyatāsaṃyata paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣyaāhārakaśarīra hai ?
gautama! vaha saṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya āhārakaśarīra hai, na asaṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya āhārakaśarīra hai aura na saṃyatāsaṃyata paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya āhārakaśarīra hai|
bhagavan! yadi vaha saṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣyaāhārakaśarīra hai, to kyā pramattasaṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya- āhārakaśarīra hai, athavā apramattasaṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyāttavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya- āhārakaśarīra hai ?
gautama! vaha pramattasaṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣyaāhārakaśarīra hai, apramattasaṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya āhārakaśarīra nahīṃ hai |
bhagavan! yadi vaha pramattasaṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya- āhārakaśarīra hai, to kyā vaha ṛddhiprāpta pramattasaṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyāta-varṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya- āhārakaśarīra hai athavā anṛddhiprāpta pramattasaṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmi garbhopakrāntika manuṣya - āhārakaśarīra hai ?
gautama ! yaha ṛddhiprāpta pramattasaṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyātavarṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija garbhopakrāntika manuṣya - āhārakaśarīra hai, anṛddhiprāpta pramattasaṃyata samyagdṛṣṭi paryāptaka saṃkhyāta varṣāyuṣka karmabhūmija pakrāntika manuṣya - āhārakaśarīra nahīṃ hai |

[209 upasaṃhāra-yaha āhārakaśarīra ṛddhiprāpta chaṭhe guṇasthānavartī pramattasaṃyata muni ko hotā hai| isa sthala para mūlasūtra meṃ vayaṇā vi bhāṇiyabvā' pāṭha hai, usakā abhiprāya yaha hai ki mūla pāṭha meṃ āhārakaśarīra kisake hotā hai? isase saṃbaddha gautama svāmī dvārā kiye gaye praśnoṃ ke bha. mahāvīra ne jo uttara diye haiṃ unheṃ mūla meṃ 'kammabhūmiga.' ādi padoṃ ke āge gola bindu (0) diye gaye haiṃ, unase sūcita vacanoṃ ko kahane ke lie saṃketa kiyā gayā hai, jise ūpara anuvāda meṃ pūrā diyā hī gayā hai|
600-āhārayasarīre samacauraṃsasaṃṭhāṇasaṃṭhie| yaha āhāraka śarīra samacaturasrasaṃsthāna vālā hotā hai|
vivecana-jaba kisī caturdaśa pūrvadhara apramatta saṃyata ṛddhiprāpta muni ko dhyānāvasthā meṃ kisī gahana sūkṣma tattva ke viṣaya meṃ koī śaṃkā ho aura usa samaya usa kṣetra meṃ kevalī bhagavān kā abhāva ho taba ve āhārakaśarīra nāmakarma kā upārjana karate haiṃ aura pramattasaṃyata hote hī unake mastaka se rakta-māṃsa, haḍḍī ādi se rahita eka hātha kā dhavala varṇa vālā manuṣya ke ākāra kā sarvāṅga-sampūrṇa putalā nikalatā hai aura jahāṃ bhī kevalī bhagavān virājate hoṃ, vahāṃ jākara unake caraṇa-kamaloṃ kā sparśa karatā hai aura sparśa karate hī vaha vahāṃ se vāpisa ākara mahāmuni ke mastaka meṃ praveśa karatā hai aura unakī śaṃkā kā samādhāna ho jātā hai| isa āhārakaśarīra ke arjana, nirgamana aura praveśa kī kriyā eka antarmuhūrta meṃ sampanna ho jātī hai| viśeṣatā yahī hai ki isakā bandha yā upārjana to sātaveṃ guṇasthāna meṃ hotā hai aura udaya yā nirgamana aura praveśa ādi kī kriyā chaṭhe guṇasthāna meṃ hotī hai|
601-āhārayasarīrassa kemahāliyā sarīrogāhaṇā pannattā? goyamā! jahaṇaṇeṇaṃ desūṇā rayaṇī, ukkoseṇaṃ paḍipuṇṇā rayaṇī| bhagavan ! āhārakaśarīra kī kitanī baḍa़ī śarīra-avagāhanā kahī gaī hai?
